¹ ["My heart



## On Abstinence

###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/1)


אמר אין האנוש מכעיס אלהיו כי אם בחשק עולם ומה שבו,
אם תזבחה אותו תהי קרב לכבוד אלהיך ואל טובו.


To be too fond of this world and of that which is therein, provoketh the wrath of Heaven. If thou sacrifice this fondness, thou shalt be sure of the glory and grace of thy God.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/2)


מה הוא אשר אדם דורש בעולמו כי אם כבוד עושר ומנוחה.
כי כל מאהב עולמו מאד נפרד מהם וידבק אל יגון ואנחה.


All man's striving in this world is but for honour, riches, and ease; and yet he who loveth this life too much is often far removed from them, and more closely bound to sorrow and sighing.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/3)


לא יפחד אדם עלי עושר הלך לפניו וצידתו,
שיגזלו אותו בני אדם אם יגזל עושרו והון ביתו,
עזוב מה אינך צריך / והצורך קחה בלבד,
יאספך כבוד האל / וזבד טוב לך יזבד.


Fret not for the store laid up, for the riches heaped of yore, fearing lest men rob thee of them, when they steal thy substance and wealth.
Leave that which thou requirest not, seize but that which thou dost need; then shall the glory of God be thy rereward, and good fortune shall be thy meed.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/4)


ביום טובה ראה רעה / ויום רעה לאל קוה,
ויציל אותך מצוק / וממכאוב וממדוה.


In the day of happiness, think of sorrow, and in the day of evil, hope in God, that He may deliver thee from the oppression of anguish and sickness.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/5)


הבט ביתרון רש עלי עושר, השכיל והכלם והתבושש,
כי אין אנוש ממרה דבר האל כי אם להעשיר ולא להתרושש.


See the advantage of poverty over riches! Be wise, and feel thyself abashed and ashamed; for no man rebels against God's Word in attempting to get poor, but he doth so in the endeavour to grow rich.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/6)


אמור אל איש הרבה מזוניו, היש לו די להוציאו לשניו.


Say unto the man who hath much provision: "Have sufficient years been granted thee wherein to spend thy hoard?"


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/7)


אל כל אדם קנה הונים, אמר(ו) לו אם קנה שנים.


Enquire of the man who hath gotten himself wealth, if he hath also assured himself of the years of his life.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/8)


בארבעה יגונות / ידבק אדם בעולמו,
בריש אין בו להעשיר / און ולא כחל הצמיתו,
וטרדה אין פנאי עמה / והטרדה תהי עמו,
וחמודו לאין תכלית / ואין יוכל להשלימו,
ובדאגה ואין סוף לה / בתכליתו עד תומו.


There are four causes of sorrow which affect men in this world: When poverty cannot extricate itself, so as to become strong by reason of wealth; when occupation leaveth no time for leisure, and is ever engaging a man; when there is no end to a man's longing for a thing which he can never obtain; and when there is unceasing care, which goeth on to the very end and finish.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/9)


אנוש בין יקרה נפשו בעיניו, מאד יקטן יקר עולם בפניו.


A man of understanding who esteemeth himself will think little of this world's splendour.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/10)


פרי אילן אנוש דומה בעולם, באבו הוא ומוצאיו ילקטוהו,
והנה כאשר יהיה מבושל, לתאוה הוא ורואיו יאכלהו,
ואם לא ילקטו אותו בני איש, יהי נופל וחיות ירמסוהו.


Man in this world may be likened to the fruit of a tree; whilst ripening, it is gathered by those who find it; when matured, it is longed for, and enjoyed by thsoe who see it; while if it be not gathered, it falleth, and the beasts trample it underfoot.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/11)


בימי נעורים ירא יום מיתה וסור מחטאים,
כי על גמלים זקנים עורות ככרים נשואים,
ברעות כבשים ואלים הרבה ימותון טלאים,
תיבות נערים לקבר שבים מביאים ונושאים.


In the days of youth fear the day of death, and depart from sin; for upon old camels the skins of young sheep are borne. When the lambs and the rams are in grazing, many of the young ones perish, and so on the shoulders of the old are the caskets of many young lives carried to the grave.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/12)


בני אלים יבקשו יום תמותת בני אדם ומה הוליד לפניו,
ויושבי האדמה ישאלו איש לרעהו ומה הנחיל לבניו.


When a mortal passeth from this world, men of worth will ask, What life did he lead? But they who only think of earth enquire, How much did he bequeath to his children?


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/13)


הלא אדם בעולמו / במקרהו ומולדתיו,
כמו אדם סבבוהו / נחשים כל סביבותיו,
במוצאיו ומובאיו / משנאים כל נתיבותיו,
ופחדתו מאד מאל / ומאלה מגורותיו.


Is not man in this world, by accident and birth, as a creature e'er surrounded by serpents at every turn? Where'er he goeth, whene'er he cometh, they endanger his every path; in constant fear, he dreadeth lest now, by this one or by that, he be suddenly attacked.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/14)


מי שימותת את / נפשו והוא חפצו
תמיד באחריתו / לעד יחיינה
אך אם יחיינה / על תאות עולם
ידע ויתבונן / כי אז ימיתנה.


He who mortifieth himself, ever thinking with delight of his latter end, will preserve his life unto eternity; while if he keepeth it alive for the pleasure of this world alone, let him know and understand that in this way he killeth the life that is within him.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/15)


בכה ודאג / לאנשי התמותות
ואל תשמח / לאנשי הנעימות.


Have a care and weep for man's mortality, and rejoice not at life's pleasantries!


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/16)


תקרא ליום ויום לעם יושב משתק ואין עונה ואין קשב,
ויעננו קול לרמותם מאכל ..... יוכלו השב,
ולמעמד הדין יקומון עוד לעמוד פני יוצרם להתחשב.


Proclaim unto people day after day the message; there they sit in silence, they answer not, nor give ear…. But when the Day of Judgment cometh, they will perforce rise up and stand in the presence of their Maker, called upon to give account of their wrong-doings.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/17)


אדם באהבת עולם רצונו, / דומה לכלב מוצץ גרמות,
דם משפתיו מוצץ ודומה / כי הוא מוצץ דם העצמות.


The man whose sole delight is in this world, may be likened to a dog sucking bones; thinking that it is sucking blood from the bone, it sucketh the blood of its own lips.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/18)


אנוש יחשק עולם / ולא יעבוד האל,
בכל עת יהי מפסיד / ויחשוב אשר יקנה,
הכי עוד יהי נקבע / עלי כל אשר פעל,
ביום יחליף סותו / ויום כל לבוש ישנה.


The man who doteth on this life, and serveth not his God, will ever be the loser, though he think that he doth gain. Indeed, he will be stripped of all that he hath done in the day when he doth change his garb, and putteth other raiment on.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/19)


בעת תחשוב לעולמך, / חשוב כי תחיה לעד,
ועת תחשוב לאחריתו, / חשוב כי מחר תתמעד.


When thou dwellest upon this thy world, think that thou wilt live for aye; when thou thinkest of this life's end, think that to-morrow it may slip from thee.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/20)


רפאות נשמות אמת הצדקות,
ומות הנפשות הכי מעשקות.


Righteousness is, in truth, the health of the soul; wickedness is its death.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/21)


כל מתאבל על עולמו, קוצף על צור רואה גלמו.


Whosoever pineth at his fortune in life showeth he is angry with his Maker who formed him.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/22)


אנוש ישיש באחת דאגתו, כבר השלים אלהיו תאותו.


The man who can laugh away a single care may be said to have had life's desire accomplished by Heaven.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/23)


אנוש בפני בני אדם יקר הודו מאד יקטן,
יהי נצול ונמלט מאנוש מזיק ומשטן.


The man who, though the world esteem him highly, thinketh very little of himself, is sure to be delivered and released from the grasp of that which harmeth and hindereth.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/24)


התעלסה על הון אשר התקדמת,
תתחרטה על כל אשר הנחת.


Pride thyself upon the gain which thou hast already obtained; have remorse for those things which thou hast wilfully neglected.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/25)


עזוב כל הון אשר חסר וחדל,
וקח ממון אשר הולך וגדל,


Leave all gain that diminisheth and ceaseth, and take thou hold of the kind that goeth on and increaseth.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/26)


אדם בעוד חי בריא בשלותו,
יכיר וידע כי גדול כים שברו,
כי איך יהי שלם או איך יהי בריא,
אדם, והמות תלוי בצוארו.


Let man in life, while in health and at ease, recognise and know that the breach may come great as the sea; for how can man remain whole and strong, seeing that Death is ever hanging at his neck?


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/27)


חכם אר ראה אנוש דואג, שאל אמור מה מחשבותיך,
אם הם לעולמך מעט תוחיל, תפרדה מהם במותך,
אם דאגתך באחריתך, האל ירבה דאגתיך.


A Sage, on observing a man full of care, enquired of him thus: "Why in such deep thought? If thou art concerned with earthly things, thou hast little to hope, for at death thou shalt be parted from them. But if thou art anxious concerning thy latter end, then I say, may God increase thy care!"


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/28)


אם תבחנה עולמך משכיל, ותתחשב חוקרו וגם דורשו,
אז תמצאה אותו כמו אויב, עוטה מעיל אוהב ומלבושו.


If, in true wisdom, thou dost test the world in which thou livest, reflecting on it, proving it, and searching it, thou wilt indeed find it like unto an enemy, wearing the mask and cloak of a friend.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/29)


הלא תירא אנוש מועד להלך בלא צידה אלי ארץ רחוקה,
ומאין כל יהי יורד לתוך בור ובית שחת מקום צרה וצוקה,
ויעמוד אל פני בוראו ביום דין, לעוברו על דבר מצוה וחוקה.


Shouldst thou not fear, O man forewarned, when, without due provision, thou settest forth for the distant country, and lacking all things, thou descendest into the pit of destruction—the place of trouble and affliction, doomed to stand before thy Creator on the Judgment Day, to answer for the transgression of precept and command?


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/30)


אל תחמוד עושר אישים וקנינים, מתוך ומחמם שמו בכליהם,
כמה בני אדם שאספו עושר אל אויביהם ולבעלי נשותיהם.


Covet not the wealth of men and their possessions, filled as their vessels are through oppression and violence. How many men gather riches that goeth to their foes, even to the lovers of their wives!


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/31)


מחסר נדבותיו וממעיט צדקתו,
מאסף ומקבץ לאוצר זולתו.


He who his freewill offerings doth reduce, and giveth ever less in charity, is but gathering and collecting wealth to swell the treasures of another.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/32)


ירא פן תאסוף לך העונות,
ותנחיל יורשיך הממונות.


Fear lest thou heap up for thyself the sins, and bequeath unto thy heirs the monies.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/33)


מצוקות תולדות ימים ואידם ומקריהם חשוב אותם חלומות,
ושים אותם כלא היו והכן מזון נפש וצידת הנשמות.


As for the worries and calamities which Time bringeth with it, and all the occurrences of life, regard thou them as dreams, and think of them as though they had never been; but fail thou not to prepare food for the soul and some provision for the spirit.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/34)


אנוש אינו בבטחה על מזונו, לעולמים יהי ארוך יגונו.


No man is sure of his livelihood, and he is therefore never free from concern.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/35)


מרדף אחר הבלי ימותיו, ולא השיג רצונו בו וחפצו,
רצונו איך משיג בעולם אשר אין אחריו חושו ורוצו.


If in all his days here pursuing vain toil, man doth not obtain his will and desire, how can he attain his will in that world for which he had no feeling, and after which he never did run?


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/36)


אשר מומו יגל לכל, כבוד נפשו ישכח,
וסר מאהבת עולם אמת רוחו ישבח.


The man who revealeth his blemish to all forgetteth the honour of his own soul; but he who turneth from the love of this world, may truly be said to glorify the spirit within him.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/37)


אדם מתקן סתריו / בוראו יתקן גלוייו,
אם אחריתו מתוקן / יאריך שנותיו וחייו.


Let man but establish his secret things, and his Creator will regulate the things revealed. If he attend to his latter end, He on high will lengthen the years of his life.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Abstinence 38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Abstinence/r/38)


שומר נפשו יזכור מותו, / ובעוד יומו יפחד לילו,
זה השער ליי' / מספר בתיו כספר ל י ל ו.


He who would preserve his life should call to mind the hour of death; and while he be yet in his day, let him fear the coming of *his night.*¹"=76, the number of lines in this section.]

footnotes:
¹ ["His night



## On Modesty and Shame

###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Modesty and Shame 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Modesty%20and%20Shame/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Modesty%20and%20Shame/r/1)


תמים ובוש פנים / נודע בטוב טעמו,
זהו אשר יבוש / בינו לבין עצמו.


The innocent and shamefaced ones are known by their demeanour; they are such as have a sense of shame even when none other is near.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Modesty and Shame 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Modesty%20and%20Shame/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Modesty%20and%20Shame/r/2)


חוס עלי יקר שכלך ותורתך, פן יעלה יצרך ויעציבך,
תקן אסמיך והון אוצרך / נדבתך לתת וחיובך.


Have consideration for the worth of thy judgment and thy learning, lest thy conscience rise and reproach thee. Carefully arrange thy stores and the abundance of thy treasure, so that thou mayest cheerfully dispense thy dues, even thy gifts.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Modesty and Shame 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Modesty%20and%20Shame/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Modesty%20and%20Shame/r/3)


צניעות האנוש זהו / בענותו וח(ו)כמתו,
ולשמור דת וחק מוסר / והסדר במחייתו,
וכל פגע וכל מקרה / בעת בואו סבול אותו.


Man's modesty may be seen in the union of meekness and knowledge, in his observance of the Law and the moral precept, in his methods of living, and in the patient endurance of the events and accidents that befall him in life.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Modesty and Shame 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Modesty%20and%20Shame/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Modesty%20and%20Shame/r/4)


צניעות איש ומיטב מפעלותיו, להתנהג ימי חייו כראוי,
ושלא יעשה דבר בסתר, אשר יבוש עשות אותו בגלוי.


It is the part of the modest man, who shapeth his actions well, to conduct himself through life as is meet, never to do aught in secret that he would blush to do openly.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Modesty and Shame 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Modesty%20and%20Shame/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Modesty%20and%20Shame/r/5)


צניעות איש בהרחק העבירות בכל עת יהיו מדיו לבנים,
ויטרח לאשר יוליך בידו לצידה ואשר ינחיל לבנים.


Man's modesty may be said to consist of his keeping aloof from sinfulness, so that at all times his garments be white, he labouring to provide of that which cometh into his hand for his own wants, and to bequeath thereof unto his children.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Modesty and Shame 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Modesty%20and%20Shame/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Modesty%20and%20Shame/r/6)


צניעות בארבעה חלקים, להקביל את פני אוהב ולדבקה באהבתו,
והשלים חפציו כל זמנים כפי כוחך והשתעשע תמיד בכל עת בצדקתו.


Modesty may take four forms:—That of greeting a friend in sincerity, that of attaching him in affection, that of fulfilling his wish at all times according to one's power, and that of being ever delighted to deal charitably by him.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Modesty and Shame 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Modesty%20and%20Shame/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Modesty%20and%20Shame/r/7)


הבושת מאד מדה חמודה היא, נראית כל עת על פני נדיב,
אכן חליפתה להכאיב (עת) את נפש בעליה וגם להאדיב.


Modesty is a quality much to be desired; it may always be discerned on the face of him with liberal heart; whereas its opposite is such as to cause pain and sorrow to the heart of its possessor.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Modesty and Shame 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Modesty%20and%20Shame/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Modesty%20and%20Shame/r/8)


אדם אשר עוטה / מעל בושת אמת
יתעלמו מומיו / ויסתתרו,
אך אם יהי פשוטו / אזי יתבררו,
על עין בני אדם / ואז יזכרו.
כל רק ומתלוצץ / ואיש לב נענה,
יתקבצו אליו / ויתחברו.


As for the man who putteth on the garment of modesty, his blemishes will, in truth, be covered up and concealed; but he who doth divest himself thereof will render those defects clear to the eye of man, so that they be called to mind; nay, more, such a one will gather around him as his associates none but the worthless scoffer and the low fellow.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Modesty and Shame 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Modesty%20and%20Shame/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Modesty%20and%20Shame/r/9)


אחיות שתים אמונה ובושת, והמה מעידות באשה רעותה,
בכל עת דברות כגלדי בצלים, ואחת בכל עת תחבק חברותה.


Faithfulness and Modesty are two sisters, one bearing witness to the other; at all times joined, as the onion's peel, one ever embracing the other.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Modesty and Shame 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Modesty%20and%20Shame/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Modesty%20and%20Shame/r/10)


התבוששה תמיד בהצנע ימי חלדך ודרך תם חזה,
יקרו חרזיו לאנוש ישב ותם למאד ומספרם אתה ח ז ה.


Conduct thyself always with Modesty and Shame in passing through the years of thy pilgrimage here, and *see thou*¹"=20, the number of lines in this section.] the upright way.

footnotes:
¹ ["See thou



## On Self Denial

###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/1)


אמר אלהים לאדמה לאזין קולו והוא יהיה במסעדיה,
לעבוד ישרים ואמונים עובדים אל נעלה ולהעביד עובדיה.


God's Word to Mother-earth did come: "Hearken to my voice!" And He did promise to support her task, if men, as true and faithful servants of the God on high, would work the ground with diligence.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/2)


מי הוא אנוש משכיל / הולל לפי שכלו,
זהו דבר פשע / יכיר ויבדילו.


Who is the man of wisdom, that may rightly boast of wisdom? 'Tis he who, recognising the sinful thing, keepeth himself aloof from it.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/3)


לא ישקטה איש תבונה / שואל אמונה ודורש,
עד שולחו תאותו / כלה גרש יגרש.


The man of sense who enquireth, and seeketh to remain firm, will not rest until he doth dismiss his desire, and drive it wholly away from him.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/4)


איש בין בעצמך אל / תהי בטוח,
עד ימשלה / שכלך בתאותך,
אם לא תהי מושל / בתאותך,
איכה תהי מושל / בזולתך.


O man of understanding! Be not confident until thy sense hath conquered thy desire. If o'er thy own desire thou canst not rule, how canst thou over others rule?


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/5)


לא נחשב אדם ישר דרך / עד יהיו נגדו משוקצים
כל העברות תאות נפש / בהם כעכברי' וכשקצים.


No man can be accounted upright until his sinful soul's desires be looked upon by him as so many abominations, equally abhorred as the mouse and other unclean things.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/6)


אסור הוא לשלש ראשים / יפרד, שים לב תבחננו,
אסור הנפש מתאוה / ולאיסורו תרחיקנו,
עוד יש אסור אין בו תאוה / לעשותו, אז תניחנו,
עוד יש אסור אלו היה מותר / נפש תשנאנו.


Things prohibited may be classed under three different heads; give them thy attention, and thou wilt easily discern them. There are things which thy soul longeth after, which thou shouldst keep at arm's length; some things may be prohibited for which thou hast no desire, leave these alone; some things, too, are prohibited, which, even if allowed, would find man's soul recoil from them.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/7)


אשרי מגרש תאוה נהיה / ליקר אשר לא בא ולא נראה,
כי לא יהי נחם אנוש ינח / לכבוד אלהיו תאוה באה.


Happy the man who divorceth his desire for the glory of that which cometh not nor appeareth; for no man will regret having stifled, to the glory of God, the desire that assailed him!


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/8)


אנוש לא ימשלה על תאותו / מאד מרה ורעה אחריתו,
וכשפיפון יהי נושך עקבו / יליד יומו ותרבה מחלתו.


As for the man who ruleth not over his desire, his end will be all evil and bitter; and, as though stung in the heel by the adder, will be what the day bringeth him forth, his pain growing greater and keener.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/9)


אם תדרשה עצה ולא תמצא / יועץ אשר בו לבך יבטח,
סור מאשר יקרב לתאותך / רשת וחרם פן לך תשטח,
כי י צרך אורב לשכלך / עת יאטם אזנו ועינו טח.


Shouldst thou seek counsel, and not find the adviser in whom thou canst confide, then turn from that which thy desire doth prompt, lest for thyself thou spread destruction's net. Lo, thy desire for thy good sense doth lie in wait, when sense doth stop its ear and close its eye!


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/10)


לולי שלשה נתרבו כל ענייני אדם, וצדקתו תהי מודעת,
זקן קשה עורף במלבוש גאוה, גם תאוה באה ולא נמנעתי.


Were it not for three considerations, things would go all well with man, and his right-doing would be assured, namely (the powerlessness of) old age (to impress its experiences); the stubbornness with which pride is decked; and the desire of man which cometh upon him, and is not vanquished.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/11)


מקבל את עצת תאות לבבו / ברגע קט ששון לבו יפזר,
וממרה את עצתה כן יכובד, ומתנשא יהי נצח ונעזר.


He who taketh the counsel of his heart's desire will in a brief moment destroy the joy of his heart; but he who rebelleth against the promptings of desire will receive due honour, and, rising above, will prove himself the victor, and be helped.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/12)


אל תהי מריב לאיש כילי / עצר ומנע הונו ממך חפץ,
עם תאותך ריב והלחם / אתה ועברות אפך הפץ.


Strive not with a miser, who delighteth in keeping and holding back from thee his wealth; strive rather with thine own desire, fight against it, and scatter thy fierce passion.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/13)


התאוה ערה בכל רגע ועת / רואה, והעצה ישינה נצח,
על כן תהי עצה מנוצחת / והתאוה מאד תרום ומעיז מצח.


Tis ever so; while counsel sleepeth, passion waketh, watching at all times and hours. If wise counsel be overcome, rising passion showeth its brazen front.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/14)


אשר הטיב בילדותו / יהי מטיב בזקנותו,
ואם חטא בנערותו / יהי חוטא עד מותו.


If in goodness the child his life begin, he will continue so until old age; he who in his youth doth sin, will remain a sinner unto death.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/15)


אנוש אנוש עד מתי לבבך / יהי ישן ותקיץ משנתך,
הנח כל מלחמות אשר בתבל והתגבר וריב עם תאותך.


How long, O man, will thy heart yet slumber? When wilt thou wake from thy sleep? The world's battles leaving, strengthen thyself, and fight with thy desire the goodly fight.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/16)


איש על יפי תבל / שומע לתאותו,
למאד יגון לבו / ירבה חרטתו.


But shouldst thou, too fond of the beauteous world, to its pleasures give way, understand, to the great sorrow of thy heart, thy remorse will grow apace.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/17)


תאוה ועורון שניהם ידבקו יתחבקו יחד ויתלכדו,
יתחברו לשחית יקר עוז האנוש, ישתתפו לעד ולא נפרדו.


Passion and blindness to each other as a pair do cling, they are caught in each other's embrace; joined together to destroy the beauteous strength of manhood, they associate always, and never part.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/18)


מקבל יסורי אל והיו כרצונו, יהי משיג לעד שמחות ומנוחות,
ואדם על פגעי זמזנו מצטער, יסובוהו כל עת יגונות ואנחות.


Who Heaven's trials in cheerful spirit doth receive, will ever gain the joyousness that bringeth with it true restfulness; while he who frets at what the hour may bring, will ne'er from sighs and sorrowing be free.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/19)


בכל דבר תשוגב(ר) אמונה, בכל המעלות רק היא יחידה,
ודי לך באמונה חן ועושר, והון עתק ועסק בעבודה.


Faith in all things doth stand high; 'tis unique and foremost in quality. Where there is faith, there is enough of grace and wealth, of substance strong, and of service full.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/20)


לא בא מזון אדם וחק טורפו לא ימצאה על רוב מהירתו,
אם יברחה ויעזוב טורפו, הוא ידרשהו עד מצוא אותו.


Not in proportion to one's haste will one's food, nor daily ration, come; should it elude thy grasp and leave thee, seek it until thou find it again.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/21)


מאס בדין האל ומשפטו, שוטה ואין רפאות לאולתו,
כי לא להועיל אל זעוף פנים כעס ועצבתו בבא עתו.


What a fool is he who God's judgment and ruling contemneth! There is no cure for such folly as his. When his time comes, it will avail him naught to show a sad countenance, or to betray anger and annoyance.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Self Denial 22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Self%20Denial/r/22)


אשוה אל נכחי / לבל יאבד נצחי,
לכונן את אורחי / אחשב בלבבי,
לתאותי אענה / לבקש לה מחנה,
חרוזיה אמנה / במספר ב ל ב ב י.


I will place God before me, that my hope be not lost. I will resolve *in my heart*¹"=46, the number of lines in this section.] to make my way firm.

¹ ["In my heart



## On Man's Confidence and Despair

###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Man's Confidence and Despair 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Man%27s%20Confidence%20and%20Despair/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Man%27s%20Confidence%20and%20Despair/r/1)


בטח באל עלי כל מעשיך ותניח בידו פעלך,
והתרצה בכל דבר יהי בא ועת יסור וישלה אלך.


Trust in God in all thy actions, and into His Hand commit thy work; be thou pleased at what cometh or goeth, so that restful may be thy home-tent.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Man's Confidence and Despair 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Man%27s%20Confidence%20and%20Despair/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Man%27s%20Confidence%20and%20Despair/r/2)


אם נועדו ימים לתועלתך, אמנם יבואון ואם תרחק,
או אם לנזקך, אין בידיך לדחות ולו ישאך עדי שחק.


If Destiny will that fortune smile upon thee, it will come to thee, though thou art far away; or if it be ills thou'rt destined to bear, thou canst not elude them, though thou be borne to the skies.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Man's Confidence and Despair 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Man%27s%20Confidence%20and%20Despair/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Man%27s%20Confidence%20and%20Despair/r/3)


אל ידאגה גבר לכל דבר לא בא אלי ידו והגיע, 
ישמח בחלק שחננו אל, ישקוט וינוח וירגיע.


Let no man fret over things not come to his hand, nor brought within his reach; but let him rejoice in the portion which God hath graciously given unto him, and he will be at ease, quiet and restful.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Man's Confidence and Despair 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Man%27s%20Confidence%20and%20Despair/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Man%27s%20Confidence%20and%20Despair/r/4)


בטח באל ימותיך לעולם ואז תנח לנפשך ותחיה,
ודע כי אין לבן עורב טרפה לעולמים ולא אוצר לאריה.


Trust in God whilst thou livest in the world, and thou shalt give rest to thy soul, and keep it alive; for know, not always hath the raven its food, not always hath the lion its store.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Man's Confidence and Despair 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Man%27s%20Confidence%20and%20Despair/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Man%27s%20Confidence%20and%20Despair/r/5)


אם עמך האל / יהיו עצי היער
עד תאמרה די / נושאים לך פרים,
אם אין, עצי הגן / לא יעלו פירות
ויכחשו כולם / עד תאמרו אים.


If thou but hast the help of God, the dry wood of the forest will bear thee fruit, until thou shalt say, "Enough"; but if He Above be not with thee, then even the garden-trees will not bring forth, their fruit they will deny, until thou shalt ask, "Where are they?"


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Man's Confidence and Despair 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Man%27s%20Confidence%20and%20Despair/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Man%27s%20Confidence%20and%20Despair/r/6)


אנוש חכם לבקש את מדתו משחר בא לארמון בית אדוניו,
ויום יום לפתחו הוא משחר ולא יכול להכנס לפניו,
יכתב לו לבאר משאלותיו, כתב בשיר מיוסד על אדוניו,
לשני דברים הנני על דלתיך דופק, והם צורך והתוחלת
כי כל אנוש ידו להשיג קצרה, צרח לבו ותבערה גחלת,
ואחכה עד כה לבל ינקמו אויבים בשובי מבלי תועלת, 
על כן אמור הן ועשה, או לאו, ותנוח נשמה ממך שואלת.


A wise man once, in quest of his suit, came regularly at dawn to the palace of his lord, and day by day at the entrance-door would watch early, so that the master could never avoid him. The master then wrote him to name his desire, whereupon he replied in the following strain:—
"O hearken, dear Sire!
From a twofold cause do I knock at thy gates,
Expectant I am, for I am in dire straits;
Sore is the heart, it burneth as coal,
When help doth not come to the craving soul.
I've waited till now my foes to disarm
Of revengeful spite and envy inane;
If they see me turn back, they will do me more harm,
For they'll jeer and revile, if my quest be in vain.
Say, therefore, 'Yes,' and something do, or utter the one word ' No,'
And so my soul at rest shall be, and the worst I then shall know."


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Man's Confidence and Despair 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Man%27s%20Confidence%20and%20Despair/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Man%27s%20Confidence%20and%20Despair/r/7)


לשואל אל תהי שואל, הלא הכל ביד האל.


To him who doth beg, say: "Do not beg; are not all things in the hands of God?"


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Man's Confidence and Despair 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Man%27s%20Confidence%20and%20Despair/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Man%27s%20Confidence%20and%20Despair/r/8)


לשאול ממך דבר אני בא, שאלתיה מאלהים בתחלה,
ואם תאבה להשאילו אהודה אלהי ואודך במלה,
ואם אין אהודה אל ונדינך לזכות ונתפררה תהלה.


To beg of thee something I have come; from God I have asked it first. If thou art willing my request to grant, my thanks both to God and to thee I will speak; but even then my God I will thank, shouldst thou not fulfil my wish; and as for thee, while praise be denied, I will not judge thee ill.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Man's Confidence and Despair 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Man%27s%20Confidence%20and%20Despair/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Man%27s%20Confidence%20and%20Despair/r/9)


במעשים הבט למעלה ממך, תכיר בחסרונך ותוסיף תומך,
ובמעלת עושר למטה ממך, תודה כי תרונן לנותן לחמך.


When thou wouldst act, above thee look, and thine own failings learning, raise thou thyself in perfection's way; but in concerns of money, below thee look, and rejoicing, Him thou wilt praise, who giveth thee thy daily bread.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Man's Confidence and Despair 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Man%27s%20Confidence%20and%20Despair/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Man%27s%20Confidence%20and%20Despair/r/10)


אשר בטח בצור עושו ישוגב וינצל ולא יראה באידו,
בשער זה דבק וספור חרוזיו ותמצא למספרם ב א י ד ו.


He who in the Rock, his Maker, trusteth, will be lifted on high; he will not look *on his calamity;*¹"=23, the number of lines in this section.] from ills safe delivered he will be.

footnotes:
¹ ["On his calamity



## On Deliberation and Endurance

###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Deliberation and Endurance 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/1)


המתון כי עת מתון הווה, ארבעה מאות זוזים שוה.


Deliberation, when occasion calls, is worth four hundred zuz.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Deliberation and Endurance 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/2)


המתן והנצל / כצבי ועוף מפח,
מחר ואז תביא / אל נפשך מפח.


Wait, and thou shalt thyself deliver, as the hart and the fowl from the snare; hasten, and unto thy soul thou wilt bring care and worry.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Deliberation and Endurance 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/3)


מתון אשר אחריתה טובה, / ממהר חפצים,
אך המהירות ואחריתה רע, / בלב איש כחצים.


To deliberate with good effect will expedite affairs; while undue haste, with bad result, like shafts in the heart of man doth act.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Deliberation and Endurance 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/4)


דרך רחוקה בחר לך, / חלקה לנוה עדנים,
ומאס בדרך קצרה, / סופה למכאוב ואונים.


Choose for thyself the longer way, for its portion is set in a pleasant place; eschew the path that shorter seems, it leadeth oft to sorrow and pain.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Deliberation and Endurance 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/5)


כל איש ממהר בדבר, / נכשל בכחו ואונו,
משכיל וממתין במעשה, / ישיג מהרה רצונו.


The man who is in haste to do a thing will fail in strength and power; wisely hesitate, then act, and thou'lt more quickly gain thine end.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Deliberation and Endurance 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/6)


אמת תסבול ואם הוא מר, / ותקרא אנוש סובל
ואל תתרעמה על כל / אשר תגרום לך תבל.


Endure the truth though it be bitter, and thou shalt be called a patient man; growl not at that which thou dost suffer, and throw not the blame upon the world.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Deliberation and Endurance 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/7)


הכי סבל שני מינים והמה / סבול צוקות זמן הרע וצרות,
וסבל להזיק רק נפשות / מלומדות ימי עד בעבירות.


Is not endurance of two kinds, the endurance which will bear the stress and storm of evil times, and the endurance which suffereth hurt in souls inured to sinfulness from days of long ago?


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Deliberation and Endurance 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/8)


זכרה אל עת יגבר יצרך, / אז תנצל מכל פע,
או אם יהפוך עולם ידו, / זכרה סבל על כל פגע.


Think of God whene'er thy baser nature would prevail, then shalt thou from all danger be redeemed; but should the world against thee set its face, remember, hail the power of patience at every turn.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Deliberation and Endurance 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/9)


זמן לכל טובה שבח והודאה, גם כן לכל פגע רעה ולתלאה
גם כן יגון לב על דבר אשר לא בא, רעה מאד חולה גם מחלה באה.


At good prepare to offer thanks and praise, nor less at every ill; a sickness sore, a grievous malady, is the sorrowing of heart for that which hath not come.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Deliberation and Endurance 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/10)


שתית סם לך טוב מדאגה, ובלבב האנוש כן הוא יגונו,
כפי גבהו ירידתו וסבות נפילתו כפי כחו ואונו.


Better than sorrow is of poison the draught, for care is locked in man's heart; according to his height, his fall doth come; the course that his descent will take doth mark his former strength and might.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Deliberation and Endurance 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/11)


את אבלך נצח בסבלך, / פן יאריך לעד יגונך,
יבחר לך מיועצים שכלך, / תשקוט ימותיך ושניך.


Conquer thy mourning by the power of endurance, lest thy sorrow be prolonged for ever; choose unto thyself the counsel of sense, and securely thou shalt pass thy days and thy years.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Deliberation and Endurance 12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/12)


יש בזמן רעה כי / כאשר תמשיל
אותה לאחרת / תהיה מאד טובה,
על כן סבול ונשא / כי אין לאל ידך,
תשקוט ותנצל / מחטא ומחובה.


Misfortune that's conquered will oft-times prove a blessing in disguise; endure then, and bear what thou canst not escape, and quiet thou shalt be, from sin delivered, and free from all guilt.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Deliberation and Endurance 13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/13)


אם יש לך סבל / בכל דבר רע
כי עדי חפצך / יביאך,
כי אין אנוש חפשי / כמו סובל,
לכן סבול ובטח / אנוש בבוראך.


To attain his desire by bearing ills, the power to mortal is given,—for no man is free like unto them that endure; therefore, O man, but endure, and trust in the mercy of Heaven.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Deliberation and Endurance 14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/14)


דעה כי אין נכבד / ויודע דעה
כמודה על טובה / וסובל על רעה.


Understand, there is no nobler thing, and nothing more discreet, than to acknowledge a kindness with thanks, and to meet misfortune with a heart all brave.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Deliberation and Endurance 15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Deliberation%20and%20Endurance/r/15)


על כל דבר מהאל בא, / גבר גבר שכלו יודה,
אם יתבונן על שער זה, / ימנה בתיו מניין י ו ד ה.


*Let*¹"—25, the number of lines in this section.] every man of sense *confess* that all things come from God.

footnotes:
¹ ["Let confess



## On Sufficiency

###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Sufficiency 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/1)


אמר
אשר יסתפק במעט / אנוש עשיר כמו מלך,
ומלך רב ואין די לו / במלכותו כמו הלך.


He who with little is well content is rich indeed as a king; and a king, in his greatness, is poor as the pedlar, when his kingdom sufficeth him not.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Sufficiency 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/2)


אשר די לו במתת אל / אנוס עשיר כמו אוצר,
ואיש עשיר ולו אוצר / ואין די לו מאד יצר.


He whom God's gifts do suffice is rich enough in store; while he who is rich and hath a store, yet nothing that he gets him sates, is poor, indeed, for he's distressed.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Sufficiency 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/3)


הסתפק באשר לך חלק האל, ואל תבט לזולתך,
התענגה עליו, ועת תשאל, / יפיק ויתן את שאלתך.


Be content with that which God to thee hath given, and look not at another's lot; find thy delight in Him, and when thou dost ask, He will grant thee thy request.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Sufficiency 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/4)


מעט מזון מספיק / ויאכיל גם יותיר,
אשרי עינו טובה / ושואל פת חוקו,
ואדם צר עין / ונפש מתאוה,
בעולמו אין די / למלאות את ספקו.


How little food sufficeth man! A man will eat and leave thereof. Happy the man who hath no greed, craving but his daily bread! As for the jealous man of lust, the world his wants cannot supply.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Sufficiency 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/5)


מעט חק טרפך אם לא תהי / בא, קבל המעט ממעשיך,
ולא תבוש ותכנע פני איש, / ויהיה באלהים מחסיך.


If small as is thy wont, thy ration cometh not to thee, seize upon the little, by thine own efforts gained, rather than be put to shame and humbled before men; let thy trust be ever in thy God.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Sufficiency 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/6)


קח מזמנך מעבירים / ממך
צורך ונזק, ומאס / פת מלך,
כי כל יקר נעלם / כחציר יחלוף,
פתע מהרה בא / ומהר הולך.


Take of that which is prepared enough to keep thee from want and harm, and scorn the royal crust; for all thy glory vanisheth, fading as the grass; as suddenly and quickly as it cometh, so quickly doth it go.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Sufficiency 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/7)


מעט ימנעך מבוז וקלון, מאד ייטיב בכל דבר ויערב
לך מיקר ומממון ועושר, לבזיון יביאוך והון רב.


A little will keep thee from disgrace and contempt; this little will therefore be better and sweeter than the glamour and mammon, the wealth and great store, that are likely to lead thee to shame and derision.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Sufficiency 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/8)


אל יהי יקר בעיניך / רב הון ועושר לחבריך,
גם אל יהי נבזה לפניך / ממון מעט ישב חדריך.


Prize not too dearly thy neighbour's great substance and wealth; nor let the little lot, found in thine own dwelling, be despised of thee.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Sufficiency 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/9)


סדר מזוניך, / יהיה לך מספיק
המחצית מכל דבר / אשר תוציא,
מזון בלי סדר, / כרם בלי גדר,
חציו לכל אדם, / ולך יהי חצי.


Order well thy supplies, for by methodical outlay they go twice as far; but without regulation, as a vineyard without fence, half thy food will go to others, and but one half will remain to thee.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Sufficiency 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/10)


אנוש בין אל תבזה בפרוטות, ומידך ללא עז אל תשלם,
הלא הדר כחול הרים גבוהים, קטן (קט?) שרביט עדי יכלם.


Be wise, and think not less of the pence; so show not thy full power to the poor in strength. Glory is like the high mountain dust; the beauteous sceptre is lost in an hour.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Sufficiency 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/11)


ספוק וחמוד אויבים לעד ולא יתחבקו יחד ויתחברו,
חד תבחרה מהם אשר בו ישליו כל מפעלותיך ויתגברו.


If two foes there be that ne'er join and unite, they are known as contentment and greed; if rest thou wouldst have in the blending of work, choose the former, and thy work will prevaile.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Sufficiency 12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/12)


חקור ודרוש והתבונן / אחי, בין
בכל חכמות / ישנות וחדשות,
היש שמחה / כשמחת הלבבות
ואם עושר / כעושר הנפשות.


Ye versed in the sciences, ancient and new, having searched and enquired, tell me now: —"Is there a joy that resembles the joy of the heart? Can any wealth equal the wealth of the soul?"


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Sufficiency 13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/13)


אם לרצונך / אין זמניך,
לבך יהי סמוך / ובטוח,
יזכור מצוק צרות / וצוק עתים
עברו עלי נפשך / ותנוח.


Should fortune upon thee not smile, let thy heart be trusty and stayed; remember the hardships and trials, the hard things now past, and thou shalt find rest and repose.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Sufficiency 14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/14)


את נפשך מנהיג / יום יום כדי ספקה,
אם לא תבקש לך / יותר עלי חוקה.


If thou seekest not more for thyself than is good, thou wilt learn to be content with thy ration of food.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Sufficiency 15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/15)


התיאשה מהון לזולתך, / יהיה לך כבוד וגם תפארת,
יאוש לבד חפשי ותקנה האמת לכל ומכל טוב והלא מסחרת.


Renounce the thought of another man's wealth, then honour and glory are thine. In renouncing alone there is freedom, better than all things; 'tis better than all merchandise, for by it thou gainest the truth.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Sufficiency 16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/16)


אין רשע כמו בצע, / וכן התאזרת,
נפשך חסום אתה / פן תהיה חומדת,
אם לא תהי נושא / במיתת האנוש,
ממך אמונתך / תהי אובדת.


There is no sin greater than greed without measure; be girded, and bridle thy soul from desiring. If the loss of a friend thou bewail without measure, e'en thy faith will in time be found to be waning.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Sufficiency 17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Sufficiency/r/17)


בחלקך שמח תמיד / חרד והדבק ביראת אל,
והסתפק בפת לחם,/חרוזיה במניין א ל.


Ever rejoice in thy portion, be content with a morsel of food, and with trembling cling to *God*¹"=31, the number of lines in this section.] and His fear.

footnotes:
¹ ["God



## On Kindness and Charity

###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kindness and Charity 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/1)


בוחר מעון בעל כרחן יכנעו נגדו בני אדם,
מגן יהי לו מפני תולדות ימים ופגעיהם ומאידם.


He who doth choose charity for a fortress will find men of their own accord humble themselves before him; against all daily accidents and dire calamity, there's naught to shield a man like deeds of kindness.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kindness and Charity 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/2)


חובות שכניך שתים הם, שומרם וחקם על לבבך,
הט לו חסדיך ומהרע השמרה לו פן יריבך.


A two-fold debt thy neighbour doth claim, observe it, inscribe it on thy heart; all kindness deal, all ill withhold, lest soon he strive with thee.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kindness and Charity 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/3)


חובות קרוב / שתים הם,
אותם תמיד / שים חקיך
תן לו העת / מנע ממך,
קרבהו עת / ירחיקך.


A two-fold debt thy kinsman doth claim; let this be thy constant rule; though he refuse to give to thee, give thou to him; though he keep far away from thee, be thou ever at his side.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kindness and Charity 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/4)


ראה כי כל מזוניך / במתת אל וצדקותיו,
ואיש מחסור ואין לו כל, / בידך שים מזונותיו.


If thou wilt but reflect that all thy sustenance is given by the grace of God and His mercy, thou wilt come to see that Heaven hath placed in thy hands the poor man's dues, even the food of him who lacketh all things.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kindness and Charity 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/5)


אשר נמנע לרחם את קרוביו ונותן הון לכל איש זר ומוסר,
כמו אשה אשר תניק בן חברתה ושלה מת ברעב כל וחוסר.


He who pitieth not his kith and kin, yet giveth of his wealth to strangers, is like unto her who giveth suck unto her neighbour's child, while her own doth pine from starvation and want.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kindness and Charity 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/6)


איש יאסוף חיל וכל עושר והון בלא צדק ומישרים,
דומה לאיש חושב להעמיד אהל בלא יתד ומתרים.


He who much wealth and riches doth gather by means not just and right, is like unto him who thinketh that without pegs and cords his tent will stand erect and firm.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kindness and Charity 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/7)


איש יקפצה ידו / מתת כפי אונו,
מלכי אדמה ישליט / אל עלי הונו.


The man who giveth not according to his wealth, but is close-fisted and mean, will, by Heaven's decree, lose power over his wealth, for it will go to the rulers on earth.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kindness and Charity 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/8)


להעלות למדרגה גדולה / מאד קשה וקלה הירידה,
רמו אבן גדולה היא נקלה / ירידתה והעלותה כבדה.


Tis hard to rise and win the heights, but easy is the fall; just like this stone, though large, it will easily roll down, but hard it is to raise it.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kindness and Charity 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/9)


כסוס דוהר ואם רזה, מרוצה מיושרת ולא יכשל בדרכו,
והנדיב במתת יד רחבה, ואם אין לו מזון נפשו וצורכו.


As the spirited horse, though famished and worn, keepeth straight to his course, and falleth not by the way, even so is the generous of heart with his gifts; another to stay, he'll go foodless himself.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kindness and Charity 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/10)


אנוש נדיב יהי חס על יקרו ועל זכרו, ולא יחוש לעושרו,
ואיש כילי יחלל כל הדרו ויחוס על יקר אוצר אצרו.


Tis not for wealth, but for name and fame, that the liberal of heart do care; but the miser will tarnish his honour and name in trying to save his glittering treasure.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kindness and Charity 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/11)


אם פי זמנך ישחקה עליך, לא תדעה אם יחזור עליך.


Though fortune smile upon thee now, thou canst not tell if she will turn to thee another time.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kindness and Charity 12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/12)


אנוש נדיב אשר / ידור נדרים
הכי יתן בטרם / השאלה,
ואיש כילי יאחר / את נדריו
ליום מ חר עדי / ימצא עלילה.


The generous will promise, and promptly pay, before men for payment apply; but the miser delayeth his vow to pay, perchance some pretext may come forth by the morrow.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kindness and Charity 13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/13)


בעת ישאל אנוש / ממך נדרים,
ותתיירא ותפחד / חרטה
השיבהו בלא, פן / תענה הן,
ולא תתן, ותכשל / ותחטא.


Shouldst thou be asked to promise a thing, and thou art afraid and feareth regret, rather say "No" than yield with a "Yea," for by giving it not, thou wouldst stumble and sin.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kindness and Charity 14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/14)


עת הן תהי אומר / עלי דבר,
תסבל ותקים / מאמריך,
כי אין לך קלון באמרך הן / לנזר ותחל את דבריך.


When thou has said "Yes," to a thing bear it, and fulfil thy word; for having said "Yes," it is to thy shame to say "No," and thus abstaining, to break thy word.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kindness and Charity 15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kindness%20and%20Charity/r/15)


עשה חסד בעוד נשמתך בך
בטרם נודך עד כה ועד כה,
והצדק לפניך יהלך,
והתבונן חרוזיו תמצאם כ ה.


Deal kindly while the breath of life is within thee, ere *hither*¹"=25, the number of lines in this section.] and thither thou flittest, and thy righteousness goeth before thee.

footnotes:
¹ ["Hither



## On Poverty and Asking Help

###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Poverty and Asking Help 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/1)


או לאשר אבד עושרו / ותענוגיו
יחד, ונשארו / חוקיו ומנהגיו.


Woe to the man who loseth his riches and pleasures together, while all that remaineth to him are the same habits and methods!


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Poverty and Asking Help 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/2)


תן לאנוש, תהיה / מנזר וטפסר לו,
התייאשה מאיש / תהיה כמשקלו.


If thou dost give to a man, thou wilt become his lord and master; be not dependent on a man, and then thou shalt be on the same level with him.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Poverty and Asking Help 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/3)


בכבדך אנוש תיקר בעיניו, / ואם תצטרך לו תקל לפניו.


The respect which thou payest to a man will come back to thee again in return; be beholden to a man when thou art in need, and by him thou wilt be lightly esteemed.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Poverty and Asking Help 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/4)


זה רש וחרם הוא לך שטוח, מרש בעוד אין לבך בטוח.


This is poverty indeed, and as a snare that is spread for thee, when thy heart is ne'er free from the dread of poverty.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Poverty and Asking Help 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/5)


אם תמצאה את שאלתך אל תהי חושב בלבך כי כבר רווחת,
יקרתך תבזה, ויותר יהיה הפסדך מכל אשר הועלת.


If even thy request thou dost obtain, think not in thy heart that relief thou hast found; for if, by asking, thou hast lightly thy honour esteemed, thy loss will be greater than thy gain.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Poverty and Asking Help 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/6)


בעת תטעם מרירות הדברים, היש בם מר כשואל מחברים.


Of all the bitter things thou hast tasted, is there one more bitter than to beg a favour from thy friends and companions?


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Poverty and Asking Help 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/7)


סבול מחסור ואל תשאל / ללא רגול במתנה,
ויותר רע בעת תשאל / ותקח חלקך בושנה.


Rather endure want than beg a favour from one unaccustomed to give; for the worst part of asking is felt at the time when thou takest the gift with a blush.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Poverty and Asking Help 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/8)


פגוש דוב מפגוש / בל מאד טוב,
ובפגוש כל נדיב / יש לך מנוחה.
אשר יבוש בעת יתן לאדם / ובדבר פיו ובלשונו ארוחה,
ולא יזכיר לך טובה לעולם / אשר עשה, והיא נגדו כשחה.


Better by far to encounter a bear than to encounter a fool; but in meeting the man with a liberal heart, tranquillity of soul shall be thine. For while he doth give to another his dole, the liberal man himself doth feel the shame of the other, his language and tone are delicious; the kindnesses done thee he ne'er will recall, to oblivion they all are consigned.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Poverty and Asking Help 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/9)


שואל ביותר מן צרכו ועורכהו,
דין הוא מאד ישר שימנעו מנהו.


He who asketh for more than he doth require or more than is his wont, will find his just reward in this:—he'll be denied and suffer want.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Poverty and Asking Help 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/10)


אשר יאריך לך מועד במתן
ואין משען לעולם על נאומו,
כצורות המצויירות כמותו
תהי מוצא ובדמותו וצלמו.


The man who putteth off the date of his giving, on whose word thou canst never rely, may be likened to a figure painted and copied,—the mere image and form of the real.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Poverty and Asking Help 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/11)


א מאנוש כילי שאלה תשאלה, ימנע שאלתך וישנאך,
ימנע דבריו ממך, גם יעלים עינו במוצאך ומובאך.


If thou goest to a miser to ask a favour, he will surely refuse thee, and be also thy foe; if he meet thee abroad, or meet thee at home, not a word will he give thee, not even a glance.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Poverty and Asking Help 12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/12)


אדם בהתאכסו בבית כילי מחסור ולא יוכל להמנות,
סוסו יהי עומד בתענית, גם הוא לא יהי צריך להפנות.


To be the guest of a miser is to suffer want that cannot be described; his horse will go fasting, left without fodder; and so will the guest, not receiving that which he doth require.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Poverty and Asking Help 13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/13)


רש רע ומר, אבל כילי יותר מאד קשה ורע ממנו,
אין בלעדיו סר לו עדי יביא אותו לביזיון ויכלימנו.


How ill a thing is poverty! Niggardliness is worse. The man who turneth to the miser bringeth upon himself contempt and shame.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Poverty and Asking Help 14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/14)


שואל מיד כילי מתן, טוב לו שיובל לקברות,
כי הוא דומה דייג הולך / לצוד דגים במדברות.


Tis better for a man to be carried to the grave than to beg a gift from the miser; for he'll be like the fisherman who goeth forth, thinking to catch fish in a desert of sand.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Poverty and Asking Help 15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/15)


צוה אנוש נדיב חבריו, / תדעו,
סודי(ו) תהיה נפשכם / אוסרת,
אל תשאלה מני שאלה כי / אם עלי
פי נאמן או על לשון / אגרת,
כי אם בפיכם תדרשוהו / בשתיכם ימנע
כבוד נפשי / וכל משכרת.


A kind-hearted man once said to his friends, let this secret arrangement be binding upon us; Never beg a favour of me but through a trusty friend, or by letter; for if thou dost ask it with thy lips, two-fold will be the loss; your dignity will suffer, and all my merit will be gone.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Poverty and Asking Help 16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/16)


שאל אם תהיה צריך / מנת איש
לאיש עשיר בראשיתו / ונורש,
ולא מיד אנוש ממון / ועשיר
אשר היה תחלה דל / ואיש רש.


Shouldst thou need a gift, ask rather of one who once was rich and hath become poor, than ask of one who was once poor and hath now become rich, and is a man of means.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Poverty and Asking Help 17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/17)


קחה מיד איש כילי נדבה / בעת
לא תמצאה (אותו) / מיד נדיבים.
הלא אריה בעת לא / ימצאה צאן
למחסורו יהי טורף / כלבים.


Only then from the miser shouldst thou take a gift, when from the generous thou gettest no help. Surely the lion will not turn from feeding on curs, if he find no sheep his hunger to still.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Poverty and Asking Help 18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/18)


אם משכנותיך לך / צרים,
אז כל בני עירך / בך קצים,
אין שם בריחים / ימנעוך צאת,
גם לא בדרכך / תמצאה קוצים.


Thy townsmen all will shrink from thee, if thy hearth be enclosed and narrow; but if no bars keep thee back, and so prevent thy going forth, then on thy way thou shalt not find the thorns (to vex thy path).


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Poverty and Asking Help 19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/19)


מים בהלכם על פני / ארץ
כמור לכל אף יעלה / ריחם,
אכן בהתאסף ביחד, אל / אף כל
בני איש יעלה / סורחם.


And like to myrrh are waters pure which run along the ground; the freshness, aye, the scent thereof, is wafted in man's face; but standing water stagnant turns, its odour doth affect the nostril most unpleasantly.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Poverty and Asking Help 20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Poverty%20and%20Asking%20Help/r/20)


בעת יהיו נכסיך מעטים, / שמור, ובנפשך אל תהיה דל,
ויקרתך והודך אל תנבל, / והעת תספרה תיו מנה ד ל.


Tis in the hour when thy worldly goods are few, that thou must beware that thou be not *poor*¹ =34, the number of lines in this section.] in spirit and soul.

footnotes:
¹ ["Poor"



## On Silence and Speaking Opportunely

###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/1)


לעולם אין חרטה בשתיקה, ובדבור תהי תמיד חרטה,
והלא טוב לך אשר תדום ותחשה מאד משתהי דובר ותחטא.


Silence is never the loser; in speaking there's always regret; 'tis far better to be silent and still, than to babble and be nearer to sin.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/2)


אם לא תהי דובר תהי מושל עלי כל מאמריך ומפעליך,
כי אם תדבר, הרשות מידך יצא והמה ימשלו עליך.


Thy words and acts are in thy power before the word is spoken; once said, the word hath power over thee, all control from thee hath passed.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/3)


מה לך לדבר דברה, אם נודעה תביא לך נזק וגם היא תכשילך,
ואם תתחרטה על השתיקה באחת, על דברך רוב פעמים.


Why speak of something which, when known, will do thee harm, and bring about thy fall? If not spoken and made known, thou still hast hope that it may accrue to thy profit.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/4)


מכאוב שתיקה לך / טוב מכאב מאמר,
כי כל כאב דבור / רע הוא מאד ומר:


The penalty of silence is less than that of speech. Who can tell how bitter and sore may be the pain of speech?


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/5)


אסור לשון ואותה אל תשלח לבל תשים עלי ראשך אשמים,
ואם תתחרטה על השתיקה באחת, על דברך רוב פעמים:


Bind the tongue fast, and let it not loose, lest thou bring on thy head much guilt; if silence doth bring one remorse and regret, how endless the train that speaking will bear!


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/6)


אצור את לשונך / כמו תאצור אוצר,
ואם לא תהי שומרה, / לנפשך מאד יצר.


Thy tongue bind fast, as thou wouldst a treasure bind; for if thou heed not, it will fare ill with thy soul.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/7)


אם תבחרה מוסר כדת / וכחוקה,
תבחר במחסום / את פיך ובשתיקה.


If thou wouldst choose the right and proper counsel, then choose the muzzle for thy mouth, and silence for thy lips.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/8)


בפי כל האנוש מכשול לנפשו, כחתף יארוב איש תחת לשונו,
לרגלה יהיה נכשל ונענש / בכל דבר ויכפיל את יגונו.


Within the mouth of man his stumbling block doth lie; as a thief it lieth in wait under his very tongue; at its bidding man falleth low, bearing punishment at every turn; and thus, indeed, he doth increase his sorrow manifold.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/9)


ראה נברא אשר ברא לך שוכן בשמים,
באזנים ועינים, ואפים ורגלים,
כרעים וכפים, חלצים ושוקים,
ולשון חד בתוך מסגר ולא תמצא לשונים.
והכין לושתי חומות, שפתים ושנים,
ולכן על דבריך יהיה מקשיב בכפליים,
וללשון תשו אוצר הוא לשומרו לחיים.


O creature, see how He who in the heaven dwelleth created thee! Two ears and eyes thou hast, two cheeks and feet; two knees and hands thou hast, two loins and thighs, yet a single tongue in an enclosure hid, but two tongues never. Two walls, moreover, He hath designed, they are the lips and teeth. Doubly cautious be then with thy words, closely thy tongue to heed, and an asset it will be to thee to safeguard thee for life.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/10)


בפי כל האנוש אורך לחייו, ימי חלדו ומותו בין לחייו.


In man's mouth lieth length of days; life and death are between his cheeks.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/11)


מה טוב בשכל יהיה עודף על מענה פיהו ומדבריו,
אכן בעודף מדברותיו על שכלו משנאו הוא וראש צריו.


How goodly if man's sense doth rule the words that from his lips do flow! But when his words his sense do rule, they become his chiefest foe, his bitterest enemy.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 12
