[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/12)


אם תחשה מדבר פחדך יגבר,
על כן ומוראך יקל [ברוב] דבר.


If thou cease from speaking, respect for thee will grow; and likewise thy reverence will fail by the multitude of words.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/13)


מה טוב דבר מסיר נזק / ממך או מביא תועלת,
אם אין זה טוב לך שתחשה, כי אין לך בו שום תוחלת.


Tis good if speech can remove some harm, or be of use to thee; where iths is not, 'twere better far to hold thy peace; speech hath no hope for thee.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/14)


איש מדבריו רב משכלו, כל מדבריו יהיו עליו,
אך אם שכלו יהיה יותר, כל מדבריו יהיו אליו.


If idle words one's sense outrun, their mastery over man is gained; but if in man his sense prevail, his words do all to him belong.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/15)


ממעיט אמריו / ממעיט שגגות,
מרבה דבריו / מרבה דאגות.


Let thy words be few, and thy faults will be few; increase thy words, and thy woes will increase.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/16)


אם תוך ביתך בעוד לילה תדבר,
קחה מוסר והשפיל דבריך וניביך,
אם מבעוד יום שמע וקשוב בעת תחשב,
טרם תדבר ראה את כל סביביך.


If in the night thou wouldst wish within thine own dwelling to speak, take heed and lower thy voice, and softly utter thy words; or even in daytime listen well, and hear; nay, whilst thou art thinking, look round and see, if the matter which thou hast in mind may safely be spoken aloud.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/17)


מכשול לשון אדם יחייב את ראשו ולדבריו יהי נספה,
אך אם יהי נכשל במועצותיו, פתאום יהי נגף ויתרפא.


A slip of the tongue may cost a man his head; he may by his own words be destroyed; whilst a slip of the foot, though sudden the hurt, may quickly be cured and restored.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/18)


בינה אנוש משכיל יסוד דברי עולם, ושים לבך לבינתם,
יתחלקו כולם לארבעה ראשים, דעה סודם חקור אותם,
יש בם תחלתם לתועלת, אכן תהי ירא לאחריתם,
מהם בלי נזק ותועלת אין בם, לזאת תשנא בדברתם,
גם יש אשר בם תהיה מוצא נזק בראשיתם ותכליתם,
[עוד יש אחריתם טוב ויטיבו יחד בראשיתם,]
על כן שלשה תעזוב וזנח, וקחה לך מהם רביעיתם.


O ye who would be wise, the root of things consider; set thy heart to understand them, and thou shalt find that under four heads they may all be classed; learn thou their secret and investigate them. Some things, at first, of service are, yet caution bid as they proceed; some things do neither harm nor good, these shun and best avoid; there are things, too, where danger lurks both at the start and finish; and there are things which are all good both at the end and the beginning. Then leave the three that go before, and the fourth head seize, hold fast!


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/19)


בכל עתים שור חוה, / ודבר האמת טוב משתיקה.


At all times keep this rule in mind: there's something still better than silence, 'tis this—to speak the truth.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/20)


דבר חכם ישיב אף וחימה, ויש מלה משיבה הנקמה.


The word of the wise will turn away anger and wrath, and many a word will restrain the revengeful spirit.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/21)


חכם לבב יה דובר ומציל, / ויציל באשר ישתוק פעמים
ובדבריו מנוחה לענוים, / ולגאים במשטוח חרמים.


The wise-hearted one, in speaking, will often relieve, by refraining from speaking, he will doubly redeem. His words to the lowly bringeth relief; they are addressed to the haughty, when they spread out their nets.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/22)


אל יבטח אדם / בדברותיו,
כי אם אנוש / מופלג בחכמותיו.


If a man be not an expert in his work, let him not speak with too great confidence on that matter.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/23)


ואין לאנוש כבוד / כמו דבר
דבור עלי אופניו / ועל יושרו,
יתן עטרת פז / עלי ראשו,
ישים רביד זהב / בצוארו.


Nothing is of greater honour to a man than the word fitly and rightly spoken; it setteth a golden crown upon his head, and placeth the chain of gold around his neck.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/24)


מדבר שוא וינצל באמרו, מאד טוב מאשר ישתוק בדבר,
וטוב עצל עשות שקר ומרמה מאד מאיש ממהר בם ונשבר.


To speak what is folly, yet know how to get clear by thy speech, is better by far than not to speak at all. Much better to appear as a sluggard, when called on to do something deceitful and false, than to hasten and join in the attempt, and suffer thyself to be broken.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Silence and Speaking Opportunely 25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Silence%20and%20Speaking%20Opportunely/r/25)


מכל משמר שמור פיך, / אז תנצל כצבי מיד,
ושתיקה לך טוב מדבר, / וחרוזיה מניין מ י ד.


Before all things guard thy mouth, and then as the hart *from the hand*¹ =54, the number of lines in this section.] of the huntsman thou shalt be delivered.

footnotes:
¹ ["From the hand"



## On Truth

###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Truth 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Truth/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Truth/r/1)


אמר
מנוחת החכמים יום אמת מצאו וכל יושר,
מנוחת הכסילים יום מצוא שקר וכל קשר.


The wise find relief in truth and equity, fools in laying hold of lying and conspiracy.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Truth 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Truth/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Truth/r/2)


השיב במדבריו צעיר ימים לאיש זקן
נגדו וכנגדו, (והוא זקן או' להכזיבנו)
ויאמרו לו, בוש שתוק, איך תעמוד
נגדו וכנגדו והוא זקן מאד להכחישנו.
ענה ועוד אם לא תדע כי האמת
(כי) הוא גדול וזקן עוד מאד ממנו.


When one young in years once rose up, and an old man opposing, endeavoured to speak, they addressed him and said: "Shame on you! Hold your tongue! How dare you stand up to oppose such a man, whose years are such as to claim our submission?" Whereupon he replied: "Knowest thou not that Truth is much greater and older than he?"


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Truth 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Truth/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Truth/r/3)


ענה חכם בלבב בר, בחלקו על דבר,
חכמה ומדע, ותט לו אוזן והוא נדיבנו,
כי האמת אהוב נחמד ורצוי, ואפלטון,
גדול ורב ומאד צורב, גם הוא אהובינו, 
משפט שניהם נבחרה לנו, אך האמת יותר חביבנו.


Answer the wise in a fair, honest way when they discuss a matter with knowledge and wisdom; give ear to such, and they will become thy benefactors, for truth is a thing to be loved, desired, and received with favour; and even though our friend be a Plato, great and exalted, and renowned for his learning, the right of our judgment we claim in the matter of truth, for Truth is to us a friend dearer by far than aught.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Truth 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Truth/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Truth/r/4)


אמת דבק ולא תשא פני זקן נשוא פנים,
אמת זקן מאד מנהו, ישיש עולם אביר שנים.


Speak the truth without favour, and respect not old age; Truth is older, strong in years as the world.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Truth 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Truth/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Truth/r/5)


חכמה נמבזה מבלי עושר, גם הוא בלי חכמה יכס מומים,
יצדיק דבר אדם ואם כזב, כן רש יהי מכחיש דבר תמים.


Knowledge is slighted, unsupported by wealth; whilst wealth without knowledge hideth many a stain. The rich man, though he lieth, is justified by wealth; the poor, though speaking the truth, seek justice in vain.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Truth 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Truth/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Truth/r/6)


מאד אתמה עלי קונה עבדים בהון יקר ונתן בם מחירו,
ולא קנה בני חורין בחנם בטוב טעמו ושכל מאמרו.


To me it seems strange that men should expend such marvellous wealth and fabulous sums on the purchase of knaves to serve them as slaves, since they might easily gain, without price or pain, the hearts of the free by mere courtesy, by one pleasant act, and speech full of tact.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Truth 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Truth/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Truth/r/7)


כל עושר מהבל ימעט, כן ירבה הקובץ על יד,
ואמת דבר, שים דברתך, חרוזיה תמנה לך י ד.


The wealth that's gained from vain pursuits decreaseth; whilst he who setteth his *hand*¹ =14, the number of lines in this section.] to save, gradually increaseth. To speak the truth be thy resolution bold.

footnotes:
¹ ["Hand"



## On Companionship and Association

###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/1)


לכל דע לך חבר ונאמן, סלח חובו ותמחול לעונו,
וחבר רע יבקש תאנות ממך תדחה עדי יפגע במינו.


Above all, thy true and faithful companion thou shouldst know, his faults to pardon and to forgive his sins; the man who is ever ready to seek offence, keep at arm's length, let him meet his own kind.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/2)


דברים שבעולמך מעט או רב דברים הם,
לבד מאהבת שוטים, לזאת התרחקה מהם.


Experience will teach thee that good more or less lurketh in everything, save in the attachment of fools; be it, there-fore, thy rule to avoid them altogether.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/3)


חבר יעצך בצדק, מסכים לשכלך קנהו,
אך אם יעצך ומסכים עם תאותך דחהו.


Adopt the advice honestly given to thee which agreeth with thine own common sense; but reject the counsel that is approved by thine own base desire.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/4)


בחר חבר אשר הוא לך וברשותך והוא לך כמו ידך,
והוא נכנע לך בכל דבר כאילו יהיה עבדך.


Choose an associate who will always be thine, and ready to serve thee as thine own hand, bending before thee in all things, as though a true slave all in thy power.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/5)


הזהרה בכבוד חבריך / לקשור
אהביו וגם (ו)ידידיו, / וגם לדבקה בו,
שים עינך ולבך עליו / בעת ראותך בואו,
להרחיב לו מקום / לשבתו ומושבו,
ובעת אשר הוא מספר / לך, תפקחנה
אזניך להקשיב / אל מאמרו אל ניבו.


Take heed to pay to thy companions true regard, so that they be bound as friends, closely attached to thee; bestow on them all thought and heart, when they make their call, provide for them a proper place, a cosy seat, and comfort during their stay; and while they speak, open thou thy ears to catch their every word.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/6)


[שנים מדובקים / כחוטים ביד אורג,
שתיקה אחות רצון / ומסית אחי הורג.


Two couples are closely attached, like threads in the weaver's loom; they are Silence, the sister of Goodwill, and the Darer, who to the Doer is kin (*lit*., "and the Tempter, who is brother to the Murderer").


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/7)


אל תחליף חבר קדמון בחבר (הוא) חדש בעוד לבו עמך ביושר(וה).
אל תהיה בוזה באיש אחד שוגה ואל ימעט בעיניך,
גם כן באלף ואהבים אין די / כספך תנה בהם והוניך.


Change not a friend that is old and dear for a new, while the old one is heart and heart with thee still. Contemn not a man because he doth err. Let not one man be despised of thee; in like manner, a thousand friends will never suffice; let thy substance and silver win them for thee.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/8)


אמת כל הון וכל קניין / יעופון מבלי אבר,
ואין מטמון כמו מוסר / ואין מעון כמו חבר.


The substance and the wealth of man, though wingless, fly away; but correction is a treasure true; there is no fortress like a friend.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/9)


בחר לך חבר בו לבך מאמין כי איש בלי חבר כשמאל בלי ימין.


Choose a comrade in whom thy heart believeth; for a man who hath not such a friend, is as the left hand without the right.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/10)


חבר בחר לך מבני עולם / בו לבך סמוך ובטוח,
וזנח באח יבגוד בלב עקוב / יבנה נוה בוגד ויטוח.


Choose among men the comrade in whom thy heart can safely trust; throw off the brother who, with deceitful heart, doth build the traitor's home, though he wash it white and clean.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/11)


האח שאר הגוף, והאוהב / נקרא שאר נפש והרוח,
עת תתחברה אישים, / בחר מהם איש לא יפתך ויכשילך.]
ובכל ימי חלדו בכל עניין, / עצמו יהי מזיק להועילך,
ובעת בני אדם מציקים לך, / יטרח בכל עת להצילך.


If only by the flesh we go, among kinsmen near we claim a brother; but if we think of soul and spirit, we call our friend our kith and kin.
When with men thou wouldst mix, choose him who will not lead thee astray, so as to cause thee to stumble and fall, And all through life with this one aim, a man will suffer to serve his friend; and when he finds him sorely tried, he'll labour hard to set him free.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/12)


היה מקדים בשלום לחברך, ותרחיב לו במושבך מקומות,
וכל עת תקראה בשמו, קרא לו וכנהו בשם חביב בשמות.


Be thou ready and first thy companion to greet, and to give him warm welcome and an honourable place; and when thou dost call him, and wish to address him, be sure that thou call him by his dear pet name.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/13)


היש בער כמו מאשים אנשים בכל מפעל והוא עושה כמותו,
ורואה הוא בני אדם ומומו יכסהו ולא יוכל ראותו.


There is no greater boor on earth than he who, looking out for all men's errors, attacheth blame to them, while he doeth just the very same; and while he his own defects doth hide, and will not see his faults, he looketh round and findeth fault with all that others do.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/14)


אמת החברים / שלשה חלקים,
לבבך תנה בם / והבן בסודם.
חברים אשר הם / כמזון לנפש,
ותמיד תהי / צהלתך בידם.
ויש בחברים / כמו ברפואות,
לעיתים ידועים / הדרם והודם.
ומהם כמדוה / ללב איש לקרבם,
יהי בא בואם / וינוד בבואם.


Companions may, in truth, be classed in threefold groups: attend to these, and thou soon wilt learn the mystery. Some comrades act as food to men, thy endless joys depend on them; the boast and worth of others seem like drugs, for certain times prescribed; there are those, too, who, like recurring sickness lurk, and bring naught but endless harm to health.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/15)


בחברת נדיבים / מציאה גדולה,
וחברת נבלים / חרטה ומחלה.


To join with fine natures is a source of great pride; to join with the senseless bringeth pain and remorse.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/16)


תבחר לך חבר / תמיד יפארך,
אם תחסור דבר / הוא יהי בעזרך,
או אם תהי דובר / יצדיק דבריך,
תמיד ואם תכעוס / יסבול אמריך.


Choose him for thy friend who will always be thy pride, who, when aught thou dost want, will be ready to help thee; or, when thou dost utter what is in thy mind, in him thou shalt find a champion bold; or, should it e'en fare thy temper thou lose, he'll bear with thy words at all times and all seasons.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/17)


זה הוא אשר נקרא / נאמן באהבתו,
חבר ירימך / במאודו וביכולתו.


He may be called a fast friend and true who, with all his strength and might, will raise thee on high.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/18)


בחברת כל אנוש שוטה אנחה, ושנאתו לכל משכיל מנוחה.


The friendship of a stupid man is bound to end in sorrow; a wise man will find relief and refreshing if he hold aloof from him


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/19)


לשוט צרך בחירת הבהמות, וכן בעל לכל אשה כשרה,
וצורך כל חכם לבב לעצה, ולא תהיה תבונתו חסרה.


The best of beasts require the whip; the best of wives their lord and sire; the wisest man advice doth need, without it will his wisdom lack.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/20)


תפוש חבר לבינך ובינו, ואם לא ישמע אל סוד ועניין,
תפוש אותו בחברת אוהבך, ואם נזהר חשוב אותו לקניין.
ואם אן חשוב אותו חסר סוד, ויפקוד ואל יעל במניין.


Between thy two selves thy friend to task do call; and if thy reasoning he will not heed, then tax him further in the presence of a third. If he take the caution, regard him as a gain; but if he yield not, think him wanting in sense; he will scarcely be missed when his number is called.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/21)


אל תוכיח אדם אחר, / גם דברתך מנהו אחר,
כי אם לא יוכיחו שכלו, איך יוכיחו שכל אחר.


Reprove not, and waste not your words on a man; for if his own sense upbraid not, how can another reprove?


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/22)


מה טוב חברת אדם מכיר / ערך עצמו גם מעלתו,
?ירה ורחק מאיש הפכו / כי לא תצליח חברתו.


How good it is to mix with men who know their worth and station! Turn far from him who's not of these; in his friendship there is no blessing.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/23)


האהבה השלימה, / מאין מדנים וריבות.
זה לך לאות ולמופת, / התחברות הלבבות.


The friendship that is perfect is always free from feud and strife; the truest sign of friendship is two hearts made one for ever.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/24)


התחברה תמיד, ולא תתפרדה מאיש יקר רוח ונוח דעת,
תיקר בחברתו, ואהבתו לך תהיה בה כל האנוש נודעת.


Always join and never part from the man of fine spirit and unruffled mind; in his attachment and friendship thou thyself shalt be courted, and in the face of all men thou shalt be acknowledged.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/25)


אהוב חבר והוכיח במוסר בכל עתים, אבל בינך לבינו,
והודה לו כאלו יהנה לך מנת ידו ומכספו והונו.


Esteem the friend who chideth thee at any time, but not in the presence of another; and let him think he hath gained thereby some part at least of thy power and possession.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/26)


נטע אהבת חבריך בלבך כמסמרים עלי אהלים וספים,
ושרשי נדיבותם ינובון עלי לשון סעפים.


Plant the love of thy friend in thy very life's soil, as thou wouldst labour to fasten the pegs to thy tent; and if thou wilt plant it with liberal root, it will be borne on the tongue, as fruit on the branches are borne.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/27)


נקב מחט אין עינו צר להיות רחב לשני אוהבים.
גם רוחב עולם כולו צר מהכיל לשני אויבים.


The eye of a needle is not too narrow to hold two friends that agree; the breadth of the world is not sufficiently wide to contain in its fold two foes.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/28)


בעת תרצה להבחין / והבך,
בלבו זאת / אשר כזב וכחש, 
שאל את לבך / כי הוא יהיה לך
כמו קוסם, / ובו נחש ינחש.


Dost thou wish to prove thy friend, to know if he be false or true, ask thine own heart to act as diviner, and the truth it will surely divine.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/29)


מי שטרחו שם עליך / גם מנע ממך טובתו,
אל תאמן בו, כי יחליק לך / לשון, ורחק מאהבתו.


Trust not the man who boreth thee with his troubles, but keepeth from thee his joys; his tongue may be all smooth for thee; his friendship's but alloy.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/30)


אהוב תמיד / אשר קדם לך טוב
ושלם לך גמול / ידיו וטובו,
וכמוהו אהוב / אדם קניתו,
בטובתך תהי / נאהב בלבו.


Love thou the man who first hath done thee good, and return him help and kindness; so love the man thou hast gained as friend, for in his heart thy friendly acts all friendliness do blend.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/31)


את אוהבי אהב / מאח בעוד אחיה,
כי איך אאהב אח / עד אוהבי יהיה.


Love thy friend more than thou wouldst thy brother; for until he becometh my truest friend, how can I love even a brother?


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/32)


אוהב הלא בלבבות / צפון ועל הלשונות,
נראה עין בעין / לבות ויצפון אמונות.


Hid deep in the heart true friendship is found, its impression is sealed on the tongue; men's hearts are as mirrors that see eye to eye, their secrets are never revealed.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/33)


אל תחניף אוהב לך גם אל תשאל בני אדם עלי סודיו,
פן תמצאה אדם ידבר בו דופי וירחיק ממך דודיו.


Play not the hypocrite with thy friend, nor by questioning people pry into his secrets, lest thou meet with a man who will blacken his name, and so estrange from thee his friendship.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/34)


התנדבה עם אוהבך עם כל עושר אשר יש לך ונשמתך,
גם אל קרוביך בהוניך, ובשכלך הטוב ובדברתך.
ולכל ני אדם תהי מסביר פנים ותן שלום בעת לכתך,
ולשונאך התנדבה עת יצטרך בצדקתך וטובתך.


Deal freely with thy friend with all thy wealth and very life; do likewise unto thy kinsmen with thy substance, mind, and speech; be courteous unto all men, and greet them on thy way; and as for thine enemy, be thou liberal and kind unto him, when he is sorely in need of thy charity and service.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/35)


השמרה מאיש אשר אוהב אותך בכל עתים לפי דרכו,
כי ממך חיש יהפך ערפו עת תחקור חפצו וכל דרכו.


Beware of the man who loveth thee when it suiteth the occasion, and serveth his purpose; for he will quickly turn his back when thou findest out his true aim and way.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/36)


לאוהבך תזרה אהבתך, סוד אחר לעולם אל תגלה,
ושונאך להשלים עמך [בא] גם לטובתו פני האל תחלה.


Impart, as in winnowing, thy love to a friend, and never reveal the secret of another; when thine enemy cometh to make peace with thee, implore thou God on his behalf.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/37)


אם תשלח יד בדבר / ישלם ו[לא] יאבד שכרו,
אם תאמר הן בדבר / לעד בלאו אל תמירו.


Put not thine hand to a thing if thou wilt not complete it, so that the reward be not lost. If thou once say "Yes," with regard to a matter, never change it to "No."


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/38)


אם תמצאה חבר אשר מומיו / תוכל ספור, הדבקה אליו,
כי אין למומיי האנוש מספר, / תוכל לחוקרו ועמוד עליו.


If thou find a companion whose thoughts thou art able to gauge, cleave unto him; for men's faults are beyond mortal ken; this one thou knowest thoroughly, then stand by him.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/39)


אהבת כסיל דומה / באמת כלי חרש,
אם ישברוהו, לא / ישוב לקדמותו.
אהבת אחי מוסר / דומה כלי זהב,
קשה שבירתו, / נחה הכנתו.


Like an earthenware vessel is the love of a fool; once it is broken, to its former condition it will never return; the friendship that is formed between men of discretion is like a vessel of gold, hard to break, and easily mended.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/40)


כלי חרש אשר יוכה ישובר לאין מרפא וכן נלוז ויהיר,
ואיש משכיל אחי מוסר כזהב, בעת מוכה יהי מצהיב ומזהיר.


With the froward and haughty indeed it will go like an earthenware vessel, when knocked, it is broken beyond repair; but the man of good sense and discretion will be like glittering gold; he will brighten when beaten, and be roused to do better.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/41)


בפעם אחת ידידות אהבתך לאוהב לך אל תגלה,
כעץ פרי יבכר לחדשיו, תודה לו ואת חפצו תמלא.


Not at once, but by steps and degrees, disclose thy deep love for thy friend; as, not at once, but month by month, the tree bringeth forth its new fruit, so confess thy love by degrees, and fulfill thy friend's desire.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/42)


השמרה מחברה רעה, אין בה חכם ותמים דעות,
כי בישיבת חברה רעה, תלמוד בה כל מדות רעות.


Beware of evil companionship, where nothing is wise or right; for by associating with the evil-disposed, thou wilt learn every bad habit and trait.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/43)


סובל חברת אשה רעה, זה הסובל גדול דעו,
כי הוא דומה לזאב יער, יחליף שערו אך לא טבעו.


The man who can live with a woman of ill-heart showeth, indeed, what is patience, and more, what is skill; he resembleth the wolf of the forest, forsooth, which never changeth its nature, though it changeth its hair.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/44)


היש נתק וצרעת כאשה מנאפת ועוזבת גבירה,
בלבו היא כמו מכה טריה, ויקוץ בה ולא ימשול למוכרה,
היש מחלה ללב וכאב לנפש כמכאבה, ומסחרתו רע כסחרה.


What wound or eruption is like unto her who leaveth her husband to lead her own life? In his heart she doth rankle as a bad, putrid sore; though sick of her, rid of her ne'er he can be. What disease can equal such suffering of soul, such affliction of heart, and grief without end?


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/45)


כשתשב שבה עם הנדיבים, / ותכבד
בעיני אל / ואדם,
ואם תשב בחברת / הכסילים
הלא ירבה קלונך / בסודם.


In making thy choice, take thy seat with the best, and thou shalt be honoured by God and by man; but if with the senseless thou chooseth to sit, thy comrades will lead thee to naught but disgrace.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/46)


אין טוב לאדם בשכל / יפיק בכל עת זממיו,
אין הון ועושר אשר בם / יתכם ויעלים אשמיו,
אם לא גברת חכמה, / תסתיר פשעיו ומומיו,
אם לא שתיקה ומחסום / לפה ולחשוך נאומיו,
אם לא הלא טוב בחר לך / קבר וכרקב עצמיו.


Tis not good that man, though wise, should gain his end always. 'Tis not wealth that hath the power to hide one's guilt from view; where wisdom ruleth, man's faults and crimes may soon be blotted out of sight. Let silence as a muzzle to thy mouth be e'er applied; and see, too, that thy words are few, and cautiously restrained. If knowing this, thou dost not heed this sage advice, 'tis better far thy bones should perish in the grave.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 47

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/47](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/47)


אדם אשר בא במקום / יקל הדרו והודו,
איכה יהי רב באדם / אחר לרעה חשדו.


The man who rusheth in where his honour and fame are at stake, dare not quarrel with the world, if they suspect him of evil intent.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 48

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/48](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/48)


ארבעה מביאים לאדם / לידי אבדון ותחרות,
גאוה ועקשות ועצלות / ובכל חפצים מהירות


There are four things which lead men on to strife and ruin dire: haughtiness, and stubbornness, indolence, and rashness of desire.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 49

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/49](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/49)


חברת אנוש נבל / כשן כואבת,
אין לה רפואה / כי אם בעקור אותה,
כן לא יהינסבל / ימי חברת אנוש
נבל, לכן מהר / וחיש לשנאתה.


The companionship of a foolish man is as a tooth that giveth pain; for both there is but one and the same cure: pluck it out. Thou canst not long endure his wicked senseless talk; for soon, indeed, without delay, thy love will turn to hate.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 50

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/50](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/50)


אל תמיר את חן אשתך / ביופי אשת זולתך,
כי החשק מתעה שכלך / ובכל עת בא לטעותך,
וכבר התעה חכם ממך, / על כן שמרה חכמתך.


Change not the charms which grace thy wife for another woman's charms; for, leading thy good sense astray, lust means to play thee false. A wiser man than thou once fell, King Solomon was his name; then see that thou art truly wise, and fall not in sin's way.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Companionship and Association 51

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/51](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Companionship%20and%20Association/r/51)


אהוב תבחר בכל מבחר, / בקש חפצו
עד / מרחוק,
חברתו שים על לבך, / מספר בתיו
תמצאם / חק.


Of all choice things a friend do choose; seek his wishes to fulfil, however great *the task,*¹ = 108, the number of lines in this section.] and thus his friendship set upon thy heart.

footnotes:
¹ ["The task"



## On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness

###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/1)


זה יקרא אוהב נאמן / המתנדב לקראתך,
עם הונו עת חסרונך, / ובנפשו יום צרתך.


A faithful friend him thou shouldst call, who cheerfully renders thee help; whether money or life is what is required, he is ready to meet thy need.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/2)


אכן בזאת תבחן אנוש / חברתך,
אם תחלוק עמו / ותכעיסהו,
ובעת גדול כעסו / יהודה האמת,
שימו עלי לבך / והקריבהו.


If thou wouldst select a friend worthy the name, try him, and see, if he'll stand this one test:—If, when thou dost cross him and thwart him, he will keep true as before, he is the man thou shouldst hold, for he loveth thee no less.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/3)


אל תבטחה על איש / יהודך בעת
צורכו, ויש לו / ממך תוחלת,
אך כאשר יודה / ויפארך
עת תמנעה לו תת / ובל משאלת.


Trust not him who praiseth thee when he be in want, and awaiteth thy help; but trust him who speaketh of thee with pride, even when thou refusest his request.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/4)


שלשה נבחנו / על יד שלשה,
למד אותם / ולא תשוב לכסלה,
אנוש עניו בעת / כעסו, וגבור
במלחמה, ואוהב / את שאלה.


Three things prove if men are wise; learn them well, and never shalt thou turn to folly: Be humble amid all provocation; be a hero in the fight; be pleased to grant a man's request.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/5)


בעת שתאהב דבר יכסה לך מומין אשר היו לפנים,
ועת תשנא לכל דבר יגלה לך מומיו ואם המה צפונים.


If lovingly thou do a thing, thy former faults will be concealed; if sullenly thou show thyself, thy secret sins will be disclosed.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/6)


אל תחשוב עון עלי אדם / יתנצלה ממך בדברתו,
כן לאשר הודה מחול וסלח / פשעו וחטאתו ואשמתו.


Think not ill of any man, if once thy pardon he hath craved; forgive and pardon all his guilt, if once his sin he hath confessed.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/7)


אנוש אינו מאמין / בעת התנצלות,
וגם אינו מוחל / בשעת הודאה,
חשוב את אהבתו / כאלו לא היתה,
ואכן את רחוקו / מאד רחקה באה.


As for him who will not trust the apology sincerely made, nor readily forgive the man who hath frankly said, "I've erred,"—deem his friendship as though it had never been, and give him now a good wide berth.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/8)


בעת יתנצלה אוהבך / לחובו או לקוצר יד מחול לו,
ודע כי המחילה מנתיבת / אנוש נדיב ומדעו ושכלו.


When, for their guilt or shortcomings, thy friends beg indulgence, forgive; for know, forgiveness is in the heart of generous men; it's in the path of intelligence.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/9)


דין כל אדם יחטא לזכות / טרם יתנצל ממך,
גם אל תחשוב עליו רעה, / כי אם יזכה יאשימך.


Judge all men's errors kindly, even before thy indulgence is craved; nor think ill of the man whose guilt is not proved, for in this case the guilt will be thine.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/10)


אל יתעכב עמך חוב איש / כי עד יום התנצלותו,
כי האחד ברור, גם השני ספק, / יבין אותו.


From the day of a man's apology, harbour not in thy mind his wrongdoings, though his wrong may be clear, and he may offer a doubtful excuse; understand thou this, and be not unkind.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/11)


לא תמצאה עצל כעצל מקנות / האוהבים אליו מאד נחברים,
יותר מאד עצל אשר קנם ואבד / אהבתם וכעת נפזרים.


No man can be found more neglectful than he who doth not choose the most genial of friends; yet far more neglectful is he, the prodigal, who, once having made them, loseth the friends he hath won.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/12)


קבל אנוש בא לך להנצל, / אם האמת אתו ואם שקר,
כי כאשר יכנע לפניך, / לפני בני איש אותך מוקר.


Receive the man who, be he true or false, cometh to thee with regret for past offence; for, in this very act of his humiliation, he raiseth thee in men's esteem.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/13)


ממרה את פיך בגלוי / אל עין [כל] מקל יקרתך,
אכן מקלך בסתר / מראה כי יש לו פחדתך.


When a man refuseth in public to obey thee, he showeth that thou hast some honour to lose; and whenever in private he offereth thee slight, doth he not show that he feareth thy superior power?


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/14)


תמחול לפשע ואשם לנבלים, / כמו תמחול ותסלח לאצילים.


Pardon transgression and sin in fools, as thou wouldst pardon them in nobler men.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/15)


לאיש תן ממך מנע שאלה, / ואם הרע לך תן לו מחילה.


Refuse not to give to a man, though he may have refused thee thy request; and refuse not to pardon, though he may have done thee harm.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/16)


הודה עונך, ואז בקש למחול על כל זדוניך,
כי אם תהי כופר ולא תודה, נחשב לך כפל עוניך.


To confess thy sin is thy very first task, when forgiveness for trespass thou seekest; for should thy denial confession preclude, thou hast nothing further to hope, as thy sin will be graver, aye, double.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/17)


סלח ומחול לפשע אוהבך, ואל תתגל בריב עם אויבך.


Be quick to pardon thy friend's transgression; and should he become thy foe, reveal it not in the course of thy contention.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/18)


אם (תהיה) תמחל לאיש תתענגה,
אך אם תהיה נוקם ונוטר תדאגה.


Have control over men and thou wilt find thy delight; be revengeful, and thou wilt find care and woe.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/19)


טרם עשות מעשה חשוב עליו / הכי עוד יש בידך לעשותו כח.
או אם תצוה להרוג איש אין יכולת בך לגופו אך לחזיר הרוח.


Before some deed thou wouldst perform, consider well, hast thou the power to do it? If, say, thou givest order that a man be killed, hast thou the power to dispose of him thus; or is thy command empty as the wind, which goeth forth only to return whence it came?


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/20)


אדם אשר חטא (ל)כל ימיו, / ויחטא פעם חבירו לו,
איכה יהי כועס, ולא יזכור / כי יזכרה חטאו וכל מעלו.


Why should a man who sinneth all his days, when his fellow committeth one sin against him, fly into a rage, and forget that his own trespass and sin are not by the world forgotten?


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/21)


אם את תדבר בחבריך / ברבות דבריך ומליך,
אל יהיה קשה בעיניך / אם דברו הם על פעליך.


If, in the multitude of words, thou speakest against thy friends, let it not seem hard to thee, if they speak against thee also.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On the Testing of Friends, their Indulgence, and Forgiveness 22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20the%20Testing%20of%20Friends,%20their%20Indulgence,%20and%20Forgiveness/r/22)


מחול לאשר חטא והודה, / וכן תמחול וכפר נא ותסלח,
ושער זה בעת תמנה חרוזיו, / במספריו תהי מונה ספר ל ח.


Pardon the man who hath sinned and confessed, pardon him in full, and *forgive*¹ =38, the number of lines in this section.] him completely.

footnotes:
¹ [Part of the word "Forgive"



## On Discernment and Manners

###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Discernment and Manners 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Discernment%20and%20Manners/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Discernment%20and%20Manners/r/1)


לא יקרא אדם חכם / נזהר מדבר בו נחשל,
אך יקרא אדם חכם / נזהר טרם יהיה נכשל.


Not in the warning by experience bought, doth highest wisdom lie; but to be forewarned, and not to fall, this is true wisdom.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Discernment and Manners 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Discernment%20and%20Manners/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Discernment%20and%20Manners/r/2)


אנוש משכיל ולבו אל ימינו, אשר יכיר לבין הוות ונמנע,
ומתנחם במה אין בו יכולת, בכל עניין יהי שוקט ולא נע.


The man of understanding who hath his heart directed well, discerneth that which is far from right, and keepeth himself therefrom; and knowing, though regretfully, in what his strength is weak, he resteth content, and moveth not beyond the limits of his power.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Discernment and Manners 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Discernment%20and%20Manners/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Discernment%20and%20Manners/r/3)


אין איש שלם באמונתו / עד תשתלם הכרתו,
ובעת יפתח פיו על דבר / יכיר לבא עד תכליתו.


No man's faith can be secure, whose perception is not sure; he will not open his mouth to speak, if he doth not understand the trend.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Discernment and Manners 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Discernment%20and%20Manners/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Discernment%20and%20Manners/r/4)


היה מכיר זמניך, / ושומרה את לשונך,
והתעסק בענייניך, / והבט מה לפניך.


Abide thy opportunity, and keep thy tongue on guard; give attention to thy subject, and consider well thy task.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Discernment and Manners 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Discernment%20and%20Manners/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Discernment%20and%20Manners/r/5)


כתב אדם וספרתו ראיה עלי שכלו ובינתו ורוחו,
ומנחתו תהורה על נדיבות לבבו ועצת נפשו שלוחו.


In a clever man's writing and talk thou wilt see a proof of his faculties, of his spirit and mind; so also a gift, and the way the gift comes, will show a man's nature, his motive, and thought.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Discernment and Manners 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Discernment%20and%20Manners/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Discernment%20and%20Manners/r/6)


השמרה מכל טובים בנבראים, ופחד מאד מכל רעים וחטאים.


Take care of the good things found in creation; beware of the evil and bad.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Discernment and Manners 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Discernment%20and%20Manners/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Discernment%20and%20Manners/r/7)


אמת גוף (בין) אנוש לעד / ולבו בעלי מומים,
שניהם בעלי מומים, / ואיכה יהיה תמים.


In truth, man's body and soul have taints in them both; why expect, then, that either be perfect or whole?


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Discernment and Manners 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Discernment%20and%20Manners/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Discernment%20and%20Manners/r/8)


אם תהיה מושל עלי עמים, התנהגה עמם בטוב להם,
תבא ותגיע לאהבתם, תיקר ותכבד בעיניהם,
תמשוך לבבותם בלי חבל, תקנם בגופותם ונכסיהם.


Wouldst thou o'er people rule with delight, deal with them kindly, and not with brute force; if respect and all honour thou wouldst from them win, then see that thou fail not their love to obtain. If thou but draw their hearts with a thin silken cord, their bodies and chattels thou'lt gain without fail.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Discernment and Manners 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Discernment%20and%20Manners/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Discernment%20and%20Manners/r/9)


העת תהי מושל בכבוד ובנחת תהי מושל נדיב עם,
יהיו כחפצך ועל פיך כל מחנם יהיה ומסעם,
אכן נבלים ואוילי עם, לא תמשלה בהם כי אם בהכניעם.


As long as thou rulest with decency and ease, thou wilt sway the good folk, the best among men; at thy word of command they'll encamp, or move on; but if thou wouldst know how with the baser and rougher to act, I say, thou wilt only subdue them by keeping them under the heel.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Discernment and Manners 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Discernment%20and%20Manners/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Discernment%20and%20Manners/r/10)


טרם אכיר מעלת כל איש, עלי להכיר מעלת גוי,
ולהתנהג עמם בטוב, אמנה בתיו מניין ג ו י.


Before I can gauge another man's worth, it behoves me to know *my* own *back-bone*¹ =19, the number of lines in this section.] and strength, and to conduct myself towards men with a kindness that alone is able to win.

footnotes:
¹ ["My back"



## On Keeping a Secret

###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Keeping a Secret 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/1)


אל תגלה אוזן אוהבך, על סוד תסתיר מאויבך,
בעת יגלה לך סודו אהובך, שכח אותו ויסור מלבבך.


Divulge not to thy friend a secret, the secret which thou wouldst conceal from thy foe. When thy friend revealeth a secret unto thee, forget it, and dismiss it from thy mind.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Keeping a Secret 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/2)


בעת כל בני אדם לך סוסו יגיד, כחד ההגדה ותסתיר המגיד.


When men reveal unto thee a secret, keep thou dark the relation and also the author.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Keeping a Secret 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/3)


ואם סודו באגרת כתבו, והפקידו בידך אהובך,
קרע אותו כאלו לא ראיתו, וישכח ויסור מלבבך.


If thy friend doth transmit a secret by letter, and deemeth thee worthy of his confidence, then destroy the epistle, as though it had never been seen by thee; forget it, and let it depart from thy heart.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Keeping a Secret 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/4)


בעת אדם לך יגלה צפון סודו ומדברו,
גנוז אותו כמו מטמון, ולבך יהיה קברו.


When a man tells thee his secret, hide both the secret and him that tells it; hide it as a treasure-trove, and let thy heart for ever be its grave.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Keeping a Secret 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/5)


אדם יגל דבר וסוד חושף,
איכה יהי נאמן ובטוח עלי כסף.


When a man reveal a matter, and lay bare a secret, how can he be trusted with silver and gold?


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Keeping a Secret 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/6)


אם תחשוף סוד לאדם, ויחשפהו לשכלו,
אל תאשימהו, למען צר לבבך מהכילו.


If thou entrust thy secret to another, and he feel in his judgment that he cannot but disclose it, then do not attach to thy friend all the blame, thy own heart was not wide enough to contain it.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Keeping a Secret 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/7)


אדם בסודו צר לבבו מהכילהו ובוער בו כאש כירים,
לא ישמרה מחסום לפיהו, כי לשונו רחבת ידים.


If a man's heart be too narrow to keep a secret, but it burneth like a fire in the range, it boots not to guard his mouth with a muzzle, for his tongue and his lips cannot be barred.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Keeping a Secret 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/8)


אם סודך לא תספר, / יהי אסירך ימי עד,
אם תחשפהו לאחר, / תהיה אסירו ותמעד,
ביום תחשוף צפון סודך לאחר ויודע כבר לבך בחנו,
אבל לא תחשפהו אל שנים, ויודע למי מהם תדינו.


A secret not told is thine as a prisoner for ever; once told to thy friend, thou art its prisoner, and thou mayest fall. When thou tellest a hidden secret to another, see that thou make the right choice; for it should never be told unto two; it is for thee to judge who is the one.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Keeping a Secret 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/9)


סוד השנים סוד הלבבות, אך סוד שלשה סוד הרחובות.


A secret between two is locked in the heart; the secret of three is one cried in the street.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Keeping a Secret 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/10)


איך תאשים אדם יגלה סודך, אם לבך יצר להטמינו,
אם לא תכסהו והוא סודך, איך איש ואין סודו להצפינו.


Why blame a man for unfolding thy secret, if thine own heart was distressed in keeping it hid? If thou thyself thy secret cannot conceal, why expect another to keep it, seeing that the thing is not his?


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Keeping a Secret 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Keeping%20a%20Secret/r/11)


כל איש גלה סוד מאדם אל אחר מני דרך יט,
שמרה אל פיך במחסום, וספר התיו י ט.


Of him who from a friend a secret doth bear to a third, it may be said that from the right path *he hath turned;*¹ =19, the number of lines in this section.] keep, therefore, thy mouth as with a bridle.

footnotes:
¹ ["He hath turned"



## On Kingship

###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kingship 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/1)


אנוש מלך יהי תובעו ושונאו מאד יצר וארצו חיש תקיאו,
ירא האל כאשר יאות לגודלו, ובמלך דבק ושמע בקולו.


Tis sad for a man when the king is his plaintiff and foe; for soon his own land will renounce him. God thou shouldst fear, as it behoveth His greatness; and cling to the king, his voice to obey.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kingship 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/2)


דעה כי דת ומלך לאחדים, שני אחים בכל משמר ומחזה, 
ואין כח בזה לעמוד בלא זה, ולא לזה להתקיים בלא זה.


Know that as one are the king and the law, as brothers on guard at every watch; one without the other can never stand firm; one without the other will never endure.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kingship 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/3)


דורה בעיר מלך / נורא, ובה שופט
צדק, ובה נהר / לא יכזבו מימיו.
גם בה יהי נמצא / שוק, וחכם לבב,
רופא לכל חולי / מכיר בכל מומיו.


Live in a town where there is a king that is feared, where there's a judge that is all-just, and where the waters of the stream never fail; where a market is open, and there dwells one wise in all lore, one to heal all thy ills, an expert in his craft.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kingship 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/4)


מלך ואש שוים בכל עניין, / אם תרחקה מהם תצטרכה להם,
אם תקרבה השמרה מהם, / פן ישרפוך חיש בגחליהם.


On one equal plane are the king and the fire; remove from them, and their help thou wilt need; approach them too closely, how soon with their heat they'll consume thee, if thou be not on thy guard!


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kingship 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/5)


אם המלך ירב אותך, / גם עיניו אותך ישגיחו,
אל תפן בו, ודבריך / המעיטם, כי לא יצליחו.


If, in striving with thee, the king views thee askance, beware lest thou face him and turn to him straight; see, too, that thy words are remarkably few, or they will fail to gain for thee thy suit.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kingship 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/6)


מלך במשפט יעמיד ארץ, כי כל עבדיו באמת ינחם,
טוב מזמן ישבע, ואור פניו טוב מתבואות רב ומלחם.


A king, through strict justice, doth establish the land, for his subjects by truth he doth lead; better is he than a season of plenty, and better than food and rich produce is the light of his countenance.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kingship 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/7)


הרע אשר במלכים / מפחיד אנשים ישרים,
הרע אשר בנסיכים / הוא המחסר נדרים,
יסית וידיח חבירו / הרע אשר בחברים,
ארץ ושבעה ובטחה / אין בה שנאת מגורים.


The worst kings are they who frighten the upright and good; they rule the worst who make offerings grow few; the worst of companions is he who misleadeth his friend; and so the land full of plenty, which is not safe, is hateful to live in; it giveth no rest.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kingship 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/8)


אם יעשוק אדם חברו, / הוא יעמוד
לזעוק אלי השר / ואל המלך,
אם יחמוס אותו, ודינו / הוא יעשוק
מלך, למי יצעק ואל מי / יזעק ואנה ילך.


If a man oppress his neighbour, to the King or Prince he'll appeal; but if the king act with rigour, and rob a man of his justice, to whom shall the man lift up his plaint and his cry, and whither shall he then turn for justice?


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kingship 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/9)


אל תעמוד בפני / מלך בעת כעסו,
כי כארי יטרוף / גם היקום יחטוף,
סור מפני גשם / אל הר והמלט,
אל תעמוד בפני / נהר בעת ישטוף.


While the king is in anger stand not before him, for as a lion he will tear and seize on the prey; turn from the storm, and escape to the mountain; stand not by the stream while the waters overflow.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kingship 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/10)


בחברת המלכים / עוז וכבוד
לאיש משכיל, / ועל ראשו כתרים,
אבל דומה להר / תלול ונישא,
מגדים בו נרדים / עם כפרים,
ובו נוה ורבצה לנחשים, / וכל חיות אריות עם נמרים,
עלייתו מאד קשה, ואמנם / עמידתו כעומד על קברים.


Strength and honour are a crown to the head of the wise man, who can boast of the king as his friend. If he be likened to the high hill and steep, where the cypress and nard their rich fragrance distill, let him remember there also do serpents dwell, they in their lurking place crouch; from thence, too, wild lions and leopards may leap. The ascent has been hard, indeed; but even now, thinking thou hast a firm footing, thou standest as though on the grave.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kingship 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/11)


אם יהיה השר / משנא לך וצר,
אל תבטחה / אל אהבת המלך,
כי אהבת השר / תהפך את חמת
מלכו והנצל / ובטח ילך.


If the Regent doth hate thee, and sorely oppress thee, then trust not the love of the King; but the Regent as friend may turn the King's wrath, and spare thee to walk securely.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Kingship 12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Kingship/r/12)


על דברת שבועת אלהים / לחולק אשקה רגלו וידו,לבל אומרה כבד פיהו / בהתבונן ספר בתיו ו י ד ו.


Keep God's command, obey the King's word, and then *in safety*¹ =26, the number of lines in this section.] shalt thou go on thy way.

footnotes:
¹ ["In safety"



## On Stupidity, Pride, and Folly

###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Stupidity, Pride, and Folly 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/1)


חמשה תמצאה בכסיל / ולא תמצאו בזולתו.
ללא דבר יהי כועס, / ולריק רוב יגיעתו,
ויתן כאשר יתן / ללא הגון במתתו,
ולא יכיר מקום מחנה / לאהבתו ושנאתו,
וסוד לא יחשוף ריע, / בעתו יחשוף אותו.


Five faults may be found in a fool, and these cannot be found in any other: he showeth anger without any cause; most of his labour is useless and vain; when he maketh a resolve to give aught away, not to the fit doth he give; nor his love nor his hate knoweth its bounds; and in time he betrayeth the secret that his friend hath not told.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Stupidity, Pride, and Folly 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/2)


אנוש בין לא ידובר / בין כסילים
לעולם ואנוש / ישר ונבר,
כמרגלית יקרה / במחירים,
מחירה לא יהי / נודע במדבר.


How little distinction doth the world draw between the fool and the upright and pure! 'Tis as the pearl of high price found in the desert, the value of which is not known.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Stupidity, Pride, and Folly 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/3)


שלשה הם מאד ירך עליהם לבב, משכיל ויכפיל כאבו,
אנוש דעת ומנהיגו משוגע, ואיש צדיק ורשע ישלטה בו.


Three things there are at which the heart groweth faint: When redoubled pains the wise attack; when madmen lead the man of sense; when the wicked hold sway over the righteous and good.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Stupidity, Pride, and Folly 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/4)


וששה הדאגה בם / לעולמים מצומדת,
אנוש עשיר כבר ירד / ועשיר יפחד רדת,
ונוטר ואשר נפשו לעולמים / מחמדת,
ודורש מעלה אינה / ראויה לו ונכבדת.


Six are ever victims of anxious care—the rich, who from his height hath fallen; the rich who, sooner or later, feareth a fall; the man that's revengeful and full of spite; the slave of ambition, whom nothing will sate; and he who seeketh for high honours, unsuited to his station.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Stupidity, Pride, and Folly 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/5)


אם תשקלה שנאת חכמים בפלס ואהבת הכסיל, תמצא
יתרון בשנאת החכמים, גם אל כל אנוש נבון מאד נרצה.


If we weigh the hate of the wise and the love of the fool in the balance, "I prefer to endure the hate of the wise," the prudent will say.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Stupidity, Pride, and Folly 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/6)


רועה כסיל יסכל, והולך את חכם לב כל אנוש זכרו כמור ידיח,
רוח בעברו על בשמים יהיה נודף, וטוב ריחו אל אף יריח,
אכן בעברו על נבלות יעלה באשו וצחנתו מאד תצריח.


He who with a fool consorteth will himself become a fool; but he who with the wise of heart doth walk, will have a name like myrrh diffused, when crushed by human hand; just as the wind, driving across the fragrant spice, will bear to every face the sweet perfume, the beauteous scent. But should the wind o'er carrion pass, far different is the case; for smells, and stench, and odours ill, will sicken all the air.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Stupidity, Pride, and Folly 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/7)


שוים בשקל חד בעיני שוטה אוהב ושונא לו, וטוב וחוטא.
אשר נגדו בני אדם בשוה, לאולתו רפואה לא תקוה.


Equal all and on one plane are friend and foe to the fool; what matters it, to whom all's the same? For his folly there is no cure.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Stupidity, Pride, and Folly 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/8)


ומי הוא זה אשר קלון / בכל עת יעלה אתו,
אשר נכנס במקום / אין כראוי לו לאולתו,
ואומרים לו שבה בכאן, / והוא בוחר מקום שבתו,
והמאריך מאד דבר / לספר מבלי עתו.


What man is he who has to thank himself for bringing his shame to view? 'Tis he who entereth the place where folly hath no right; who will perforce his own seat choose, when once his seat's assigned; who talketh too much, and, what is worse, talketh out of time.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Stupidity, Pride, and Folly 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/9)


כסיל עת תם תשובותיו / יהי מוחל גדפותיו.


When a fool has had his say, of his swearing take no note.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Stupidity, Pride, and Folly 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/10)


אנוש לא יהיה גאה בנפשו, בכל פעם ופעם יעזרנה,
וישפל האנוש גאה ונשא עדי עפר, וכל איש ירמסנה.


The man all meek, who cannot boast, will betimes receive a brother's help; but the high and haughty will be abased, and trampled in the dust.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Stupidity, Pride, and Folly 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/11)


אדם בבינתו נשען ובעצתו, 
תעה ונכשל בטח בחכמתו.


He doth err who on his wit and counsel doth rely; he will surely fall, who trusteth to his sense alone.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Stupidity, Pride, and Folly 12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/12)


אתמה על כל אדם משכיל איכה אין אל לבו נותן,
איך יתגאה אדם עבר זה פעמים מעבר שתן.


I wonder why, O man of reason, thou settest not thy heart to think: "Why be vain?" "Why proudly boast? Dost thou forget thy origin—Nature's part in the begetting?"


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Stupidity, Pride, and Folly 13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/13)


איש גאוה לובש / בשכלו יחזה
גדול בנשמתו / ורב ממנה,
ישמח ללא דבר / ויתגאה, ועת
יחשוב לעלותה / וישפילנה.


The man, in senseless pride bedecked, deems his own soul too dear; boasting his powers beyond all due, he congratulates himself; but from the time he thinks he is rising, he will be sure to find that he is being brought down low.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Stupidity, Pride, and Folly 14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Stupidity,%20Pride,%20and%20Folly/r/14)


עם הכסיל אל תדרכה תוך גל,
ועת תאבה לספור מספר חרוזיה תמצא במניין ג ל.


Be not led by a fool to tread into the *heap*¹ =33, the number of lines in this section.] (and so bespatter thyself).

footnotes:
¹ ["Heap"



## On Jealousy

###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Jealousy 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Jealousy/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Jealousy/r/1)


קנאה מאד רעה ומכה נחלה לגוף, ועצמותיו תהי שוברת,
שנאה תהי מעלים לעולמים, והקנאה לעולם בך תהי נכרת.


How great an ill is jealousy! How sickening a sore! It breaketh the very bones of man, and destroyeth his body soon. Hatred may be kept from view for ever and for aye; but jeaslousy is discerned at once; to all men it is clear.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Jealousy 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Jealousy/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Jealousy/r/2)


כל האנוש תוכל לרצות חוץ מאיש עלי עושרך מקנא בך,
כי לא מרוצה יהיה לבו עד יאבדה עושרך וכל טובך.


Thou mayest succeed in pleasing all save him who envieth thee thy riches; for his heart will never be content, till thou hast lost thy happiness and wealth.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Jealousy 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Jealousy/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Jealousy/r/3)


לא תמצאה חוטא איש קינא על איש אשר ידו יקר מצא, 
כי הוא כנגד בוראו קוצף תמיד, ושונה באשר רצה.


Thou canst not find a sinner greater than the man who hateth one to whom honours have come; for continually upon his Creator he poureth forth his wrath, chafed at what He hath willed as the other man's lot.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Jealousy 4
