[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Jealousy/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Jealousy/r/4)


אויל יום תקראה אותו לעזרה לשרש גזעך אמנם קראתו,
ולולי עצי יער אשר תחזיק יד איש בקרדום לא כרתו.


On the day thou callest a fool to thy help, thou dost invite him to destroy thee root and branch; for were it not that there are trees in the forest, the axe which is seized by the hand of man would indeed have nothing to hew.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Jealousy 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Jealousy/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Jealousy/r/5)


אין לך שנאות מאין רפואות,
כי אם שנאות מצד קנאות.


A cure there is for every form of hate, save for the hate that springeth from envy.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Jealousy 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Jealousy/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Jealousy/r/6)


לא יאריך ימים / מקנא באשר
לא לו תהי תקוה / ולא תוחלת,
ידאג ודאגתו תמידה / על אשר
לא לו תהי נזק / ולא תועלת.


The envious one will not have length of days, since he hath neither hope nor expectation; he will grieve, and his grief will be unending, if he find that he can do no further harm unto others.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Jealousy 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Jealousy/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Jealousy/r/7)


מקנא אל תהי באנוש יברך ימי חייו במאכל תענוגות,
למען ישבעה הוא מעדנים, ואתה תשבעה נצח דאגות.


Envy not one blessed all the days of his life with the enjoyment of pleasure, when thou seest him happy and full of delight, whilst thou art ever full of care and sorrow.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Jealousy 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Jealousy/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Jealousy/r/8)


שלשה האדם מקנא בם ימיו, ועינו צר בהם וירבה שטמתו, 
באיש אומנותו ואיש ממשפחתו ואיש בשכונתו בגודל יקרתו.


How foolish the spite, the ill-will how petty! Three classes there are whom men envy the most: the men of their craft, their relatives, and their friends, who outstrip them in honour and greatness.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Jealousy 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Jealousy/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Jealousy/r/9)


אם לא תהי קנאי / תרום ותשא הוד,
תגל ותצליח, / בתיו כמניין ה ו ד.


Be thou not envious, and thou shalt rise, and *honour*¹ =15, the number of lines in this section.] gain; thou shalt rejoice and prosper.

footnotes:
¹ ["Honour"



## On Hypocrisy, Slander, and Evil Neighbors

###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Hypocrisy, Slander, and Evil Neighbors 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/1)


אשר יתקשטה / מאין נכונה,
אמת יתגל / בעדי הבחינה.
ומתקשט באין טבע / בעל כרחו יגלהו,
וצובע שער ראשו, / חלישותו תעידהו.


He who adorneth himself with ornaments which are not proper or right, will live to see the test applied, and the truth will be revealed. If a man deck himself with something far from natural, when least expected he will expose the fleck. 'Tis just as when a man dyeth his hair, the weak parts which he intends to hide will be sure to tell against him.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Hypocrisy, Slander, and Evil Neighbors 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/2)


חבר ולבו רע דומה לקדחת, יתפשטה בגו לחלוש בריאותו,
כי הוא יחפש על מומי חבירו, גם יעלים ויסתור את טובו וצדקתו.


Like a fever that spreadeth through the frame of a man the health of his body to spoil, is the companion whose heart is full of ill-will; for while he seeketh to speak of his friend's failures and faults, his good points and merits he taketh care to obscure.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Hypocrisy, Slander, and Evil Neighbors 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/3)


בעת תשמעה אדם ידבר באוהביך דבר מום וכל דופי, והוא לא ידבר בו,
אהוביך שמור, ושנא בנרגן אשר דרכו לדבר באישים, כי שנאות ושוא חרבו.


When thou hearest a person reviling thy friend, some failing exposing, or speaking some slander, while thy friend never speaketh a word against him; then draw thy friend closer to thee, and look on the slanderer with horror and scorn, for his way is to speak against men; the wickedness of hatred and baseness of falsehood are the weapons he calleth to aid.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Hypocrisy, Slander, and Evil Neighbors 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/4)


אם תחזה נאמן נראה בעיניך מגיד דבר דופי אל תאמן עדיו,
כי אם יהי נאמן, לא יהי מגיד דופי ושמע שוא לעד לבלעדיו.


The man once thought trustworthy by thee, who hath been found guilty of slander, trust nevermore his word or oath; for had he been the man worthy of thy trust, he would have been loath ever to utter to another the slanderous and false report.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Hypocrisy, Slander, and Evil Neighbors 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/5)


אל תאמן כל מדברות אדם, כי אין אמת כל מה בפה דובר,
איש יאמין רכיל ונרגן לא ישאיר לנפש אח ולא חבר.


Have no faith in all that men do say, for untruthful speech is oft the outcome of man's lips; he who trusteth the slanderer who evil tales doth bear, will find ere long he hath neither kin nor a single friend left.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Hypocrisy, Slander, and Evil Neighbors 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/6)


אל תחשוף סתרי חבריך, פן יחשוף האל סתריך.


Disclose not that which is bound by friendship's secrecy, lest God the secret things concerning thee reveal.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Hypocrisy, Slander, and Evil Neighbors 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/7)


היה ענו שפל רוח ודכא לעולם, ועזוב חימה וחרף,
ואל תגד דבר חירוף לאדם, ודע כי המספר הוא מחרף.


In spirit be meek, humble, and low, ever shunning all anger and slander; besmirch no man through ill report; know, that the tale-bearer is oft the tale-maker.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Hypocrisy, Slander, and Evil Neighbors 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/8)


שכן אשר לבו לרעה בך, סור משכונתו ורחקנה,
אם יחזה טובה יכסנה, אם יחזה רעה יגלינה.


As for him who would thy comrade be, whose heart is ill-disposed, remove from him, keep far away, be never close to him; for while he keepeth hid and concealed the good that thou doest, he taketh care that all thy failings shall well be brought to light.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Hypocrisy, Slander, and Evil Neighbors 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/9)


אנוש חמס בכל עת ישנאה טוב ויבחר רע ומדיינים ומרי,
כמעשה הזבוב נגע מחפש, ומקום יעזוב שלם ובריא.


The bully at all times will hate what is good, and choose evil, rebellion, and strife; like the fly which seeketh out the wound that is sore, avoiding the spot that is healthy and sound.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Hypocrisy, Slander, and Evil Neighbors 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/10)


אנוש סובל / בהזיק לו שכנו,
יהי נוחל / כבוד ביתו והונו,
אנוש רוצה להנקם / נקמה על משנאהו,
לנפשו מעלה יוסיף / ואז עקב ינורהו.


The man who endureth the injury done to him by his neighbour, will in time possess that neighbour's house and all of his belongings. The best revenge that man can will to avenge him of his foe, is to strengthen within him all that's good, and his reward shall dwell with him.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Hypocrisy, Slander, and Evil Neighbors 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/11)


אדם משנא חבירו / בפה ובלב יחטא,
יניר ויחרוש ויזרע / שנאה, ויקצור חרטה.


In hating thy fellow, what sins do thy mouth and heart upon thee bring! Tilling, and ploughing, and sowing hate, naught but regret thou shalt reap.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Hypocrisy, Slander, and Evil Neighbors 12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/12)


היה נשמר, ואדם אל / יהי נקלה בעיניך,
ויש אנוש זבוב קטן / יהי מחנק גרוניך.


Beware, and treat no man with scorn, nor let him feel thy slight; a tiny fly may choke a man, having seized him by the throat.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Hypocrisy, Slander, and Evil Neighbors 13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Hypocrisy,%20Slander,%20and%20Evil%20Neighbors/r/13)


מרחיק עצמו משכן רע, אז צור ישעו מיד יחז,
לא יירא מלשון הרע, מספר בתיו תמצאם ח ז.


Who from an evil companion his distance shall keep, his Rock of Salvation is sure to *behold*¹ =15, the number of lines in this section.] and so the tongue of the slanderer he need never fear.

footnotes:
¹ ["Behold"



## On Visiting Friends and the Sick, and on Nuisances in Visiting

###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Visiting Friends and the Sick, and on Nuisances in Visiting 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/1)


איש מניח בקור אוהב דבק אליו וחומס נפשו,
אך איש בקר אדם כבד ימצא קלון שיבאישו.


He who faileth to visit his intimate friend doeth violence to his soul; but he whose dull visit is one heavy wail, will meet with confusion, insult, and shame.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Visiting Friends and the Sick, and on Nuisances in Visiting 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/2)


בקר לעתים רחוקות / תוסיף בלבות אהבות,
אך אם לעתים קרובות, / תוסיף שנאות ואיבות.


Visit but rarely, attachment will grow; from visits too frequent estrangement will spring.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Visiting Friends and the Sick, and on Nuisances in Visiting 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/3)


מיל תטרחה עת תבקר / אנשי חלאים ואונים,
וטרח שנים להכניס / שלום ולשבית מדנים.


Let not a mile be the proverbial stile, when thou shouldst visit the sorrowing and sick; let two miles be as nothing, when as peacemaker thou'rt sought, to cause strife and dissension to cease among men.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Visiting Friends and the Sick, and on Nuisances in Visiting 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/4)


חדל מבקרה כל / עת חברים,
והוקר רגלך / ממשכניהם,
ראה מטר / בהעצר וגשם,
בני אדם בעוד / יכלון עליהם.


Visit not thy friends at all times and all hours, but keep back thy foot from their dwelling; see, how men long for the showers of rain, when for a time they are kept back from the earth!


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Visiting Friends and the Sick, and on Nuisances in Visiting 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/5)


כבד לבקר בא / פני חולה,
ויאריך עמו / את ישיבתו,
ויענה לו, אם לך / עסק, מהר
להשלימו / ולעשותו,
אך אם אני חייב לך / מחוב, אמור
כמה אני חייב, / (שאל) וקח אותו.


When a bore once came to visit a friend, prolonging his stay to an inordinate length, the patient remarked—"If thou hast business to do, then say so, be quick! Let's be done with the thing! Or if it's a debt thou hast come to claim, then speak of the debt. How much do I owe? I'll give it: Take it and go!"


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Visiting Friends and the Sick, and on Nuisances in Visiting 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/6)


קחה מוסר לך ייטיב וינעם, ואיש כבד חדל מבקרהו,
למען תהיה עליו למשא בבקורך מאד ממחלהו.


O dullard, if thou'lt be advised for thy good, pray visit the sick no longer; for worse, worse by far than the sickness itself, are the visits that prove thee a bore.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Visiting Friends and the Sick, and on Nuisances in Visiting 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/7)


אמרו לחולה מה חולייך, ענם זדון נפשי וחטאתי,
ויאמרו לו, תאות לבך מה היא, השיבם רק היא סליחתי.
אמרו הנלך נקראה רופא, ויענם הוא החלה אותי,
השיבוהו נאכילך, ענם כל מזון הוא מחלתי.


A patient was once asked, "What aileth thee?" And he answered forthwith, "My will, that is stubborn and sinful." They said unto him then, "What is the wish that thou wouldst now ask?" And he answered and said, "My sole wish is that Heaven may forgive me my sins." Again they did ask, "Shall we go and fetch the physician?" And he answered and said, "'Twas he who made me ill." They continued and spake, "Shall we give you some relish?" And his answer was then, "I am sick of all food."


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Visiting Friends and the Sick, and on Nuisances in Visiting 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/8)


דחה כד בחברתך, / במיעוט האזנתך.


To keep off the bore from thy circle of friends, have little to do with him, give him no ear.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Visiting Friends and the Sick, and on Nuisances in Visiting 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/9)


אכילה עם אהוביך / כנופת צוף ומרקחת,
אכילה עם משנאיך / כספחת וקדחת.


Like tasting of honeycomb's droppings, is the pleasure of dining with friends; to dine with men that are hateful to thee, is as bad as the fever and scab.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Visiting Friends and the Sick, and on Nuisances in Visiting 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/10)


בעת תראה בסוד / יושבים שנים מבני אדם,
רחק מהם, ואל תבחר / להכנס בתוך סודם.


When thou seest two in close converse, quickly withdraw and hold back; obtrude not thyself on their presence.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Visiting Friends and the Sick, and on Nuisances in Visiting 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/11)


המאריך אומרו ושיחתו, / קצים בני אדם בחברותו.


The world grows sick of the society of those who never cease to prate.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Visiting Friends and the Sick, and on Nuisances in Visiting 12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/12)


מלך היה שונא כבד, ויצו לכתוב על טבעתו,
הכבדת קום, ויהי לו הטבעת עד לכתו.


A King, to get rid of the bore he did hate, bethought him of this fine device: He bade them inscribe on the signet he wore these words which bluntly told him his mind—"Canst thou not see thou art a great bore? O dull one, rise and begone!" And this ring he would turn again and again, till the bore rose up and went off.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Visiting Friends and the Sick, and on Nuisances in Visiting 13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/13)


הלא כתוב בספר הרפואות באגרת שמינית גם תשיעית,
אשר מושב אנוש כבד בחברה, ישיבתו כקדחת רביעית.


Is it not writ in the 8th and 9th chapters of Medical Lore, that, as the quaternary fever is the visit of the heavy and dull?


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Visiting Friends and the Sick, and on Nuisances in Visiting 14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/14)


אנוש כבד יבקרך / בפעם ובאחרת,
ולו לשון ברוב דבר / ושונה ומדברת.
תשו אזנך כחרשת / ועינך שים כעורת,
ורגליך כגדמת, / ופיך שים כסוררת.


When a dull man visiteth thee at one time or another, and, stupidly chatting on a host of things, repeateth himself in his talk, then make thyself deaf, and pretend to be blind, act as though thou hast lost the use of thy feet, and as though thy mouth, in rebellion, had resolved not to speak.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, On Visiting Friends and the Sick, and on Nuisances in Visiting 15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/On%20Visiting%20Friends%20and%20the%20Sick,%20and%20on%20Nuisances%20in%20Visiting/r/15)


בקר אוהב גם איש חולה, וחדל לך מלבקר הכבד,
אם תתבונן בחרוזיהם, תמצאם מניין ה כ ב ד.


Visit a friend, visit the sick; but, pray, cease thou from visiting at all, if thou must needs be *heavy*¹ =31, the number of lines in this section.] and dull.

footnotes:
¹ ["Heavy"



## Last Chapter of the Book; Collection of Maxims

###### Shekel HaKodesh, Last Chapter of the Book; Collection of Maxims 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/1)


אל תהיה בוכה / פני שוחק ושר,
כי לך יהיה לועג / וגם יבזך.
ולאיש בלי יגון / יגונך אל תגל,
כי לא לך מביט / ולא יחזך.


Before the tyrant or ruler shed not thy tears, for not alone will he mock thee, but he will also despise thee; so to the man without troubles reveal not thy sorrow, askance he'll look at thee, or he may not even regard thee.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, Last Chapter of the Book; Collection of Maxims 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/2)


עצה שאל מכל / זקן וכל נער,
יש עת אשר תמצא / עצה בלב נער.


Be ready to seek advice from all, whether they be young or old; at times thou mightest think it well to act on advice that cometh from a boor.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, Last Chapter of the Book; Collection of Maxims 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/3)


מקום גרו אבותיך היה גר, ושימה את מלונך בנינו,
ותן מקום עלי יושביו לעולם, וכן נמצא כבוד העוף בקנו.


Reside in the place where thy fathers have dwelt, and set up thy dwelling in their old habitation; there's a place in the world for all who would dwell in it; e'en the bird nestles best in its own favourite nest.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, Last Chapter of the Book; Collection of Maxims 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/4)


לעד לאשה אל תהי גולה סוד פן תביאך לבזיון,
את נפשך שמרה, וסם המות אל תהי שותה לנסיון.


To a woman a secret thou shouldst never disclose, lest for thy pains it bring thee derision; if thou dost prize dearly the life that is thine, quaff not the poison,—it's too great a trial.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, Last Chapter of the Book; Collection of Maxims 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/5)


אשר יפקיד נכסים / על ידי נער וגם אשה,
בראשיתו תהי בושה, ואחריתו לבקשה.


He who entrusteth his possessions to his child or to his wife, will sure come to beggary, having first come to shame.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, Last Chapter of the Book; Collection of Maxims 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/6)


ומוכיח לאיש אינו מקבל והדובר על לבות סתומים,
כמו אדם ישורר שיר למתים, ומתפלל וצועק לצלמים.


He that chideth him who refuseth to take sound advice, who speaketh to hearts that are closed, is like one who singeth a song to the dead, or crieth in prayer to the shades.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, Last Chapter of the Book; Collection of Maxims 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/7)


לאיש משכיל מעט הוכח / ויועיל, ויבין בדברים עד כלותם,
ויקבעם למשפט לא / רחקה, ויבין את קצתם מקצתם.


The man of sense needs but few words whereby he may profit, and be corrected; understanding well, he will see the purpose of things; he will fix them deep within his heart, and grasp them from end to end.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, Last Chapter of the Book; Collection of Maxims 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/8)


האיש אשר ירבה שחוק פיהו שוטה, ואין רפאות לסכלתו,
העת אשר שניו יגלה, את הודו יהי ממעיט ופסדתו.


The man always laughing is a fool, and for his folly there is no cure and relief; at the time when he showeth his teeth, he loseth his dignity and honour.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, Last Chapter of the Book; Collection of Maxims 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/9)


הארך המחשבתך ואז יהיו כל מפעלותיך במכלולים,
כי מעדני גן בהתאחר יהיו מתוקים גם מבושלים.


Take a long time to make up thy mind, and then shall thy actions be perfectly sound; for the garden-fruit that is late in appearing will always be sweeter and far more mature.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, Last Chapter of the Book; Collection of Maxims 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/10)


איש בין לנצח ברשע אל תשו כח ואמץ בו והעז מצח,
כי המנצח ברשתו יפול, גם המנוצח הוא אמת נוצח.


Spend not thy strength, O wise one, in the attempt to overcome the wicked; but being strong and all-daring, oppose a bold front; for oft in his net the victor will fall, and the conquered will truly the conqueror be named.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, Last Chapter of the Book; Collection of Maxims 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/11)


אנוש סכל יקדם עת ידבר לשונו את פני שכלו לסכלו,
ואיש משכיל בהתאחר לעולם לשונו אחרי דעו ושכלו.


In speaking, a stupid man will send his tongue in advance of his good sense; but a man of sense will check his tongue, and let prudence keep it back.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, Last Chapter of the Book; Collection of Maxims 12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/12)


שחוק לכסיל עשות זימה ועל כן תבונתו תהי אחר לשונו,
והמשכיל יאחר את דבריו, ולא יחל בדבר עד יבינו.


The fool in mischief findeth his sport; hence his reason cometh after his tongue; but the man of sense delayeth his words, and beginneth no matter until he hath grasped it.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, Last Chapter of the Book; Collection of Maxims 13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/13)


אשר יבטיחך יום יום במתן, בתוחלת מדכא לב ומחלה,
כענן מבלי גשם ומטר יכסה עין אדמה באפילה.


He who promiseth a gift from day to day, sickeneth the heart with hope deferred; as the lowering clouds, that rainless pass, cover the face of the earth with gloom.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, Last Chapter of the Book; Collection of Maxims 14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/14)


בגרות אם תהי נפשך וריקם תפחד, שוב קחה מוסר ותערים,
והמעיטה לך אכול ושתה, מעט בשר הלא די לכשרים,
ואל תרבה נשים, וכל עת שמור פן תתנה חילך לזרים,
וכל דבר תאוה נפשך בו, אמר עליו מאד דמיו יקרים,
הלא טוב לעזוב עושר לאויבים לרוב הון משאל מן העשירים.


If thy soul shall fulness have, and poverty thou dost fear, then turn about, and take advice, and show thyself truly wise: of food take less, and duly shun the vice of gluttony; how little meat for proper folk will readily suffice! Too many wives, in the next place, see that thou dost avoid, and be on thy guard, lest thou dost sin, and thy strength unto strangers give. Whene'er thy soul desireth some object to attain, think, is not the price thou pay'st for it much too high and dear? Surely it is better by far to leave of thy riches to thy foes, than, recklessly spending all that thou hast, to beg cold charity of the wealthy.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, Last Chapter of the Book; Collection of Maxims 15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/15)


עת הצלחת איש תשוב לו אבן חוצות שן ובדולח,
ובעת תשלח על מצליח תוך ידו עץ יבש שב לח,
האמן גם על נטע נטע, אם בן יומו פארות ישלח.


When kindly fortune waiteth on man, the very stones on the road shall yield him bdellium and gold; and if it be destined that he shall succeed, the branch that is dry shall blossom in his hand; be assured and believe that on the day he doth sow, beautiful branches will bud and spring forth.


###### Shekel HaKodesh, Last Chapter of the Book; Collection of Maxims 16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/16)


אם תשמע מוסר תזריח, תנוח נפש חן חן לה,
בצרור החיים מושבה, מניין בנין בתיו תמצאם ל ה.


If thou listen to counsel, thou'lt shine forth bright, and thy soul shall be at ease *with herself,*¹ =35, the number of lines in this section.] finding favour with all.

footnotes:
¹ ["With herself"

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Shekel%20HaKodesh/s/Last%20Chapter%20of%20the%20Book%3B%20Collection%20of%20Maxims/r/18)


סליק כולו בע"ה.
ברוך נותן ליעף כח ול"א ע"י.


***Finished with the help of God. Blessed be He who giveth power to the weary, and to him who hath no strength He increaseth strength.***
